### [1] [Massless neutrinos in a left-right symmetric universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31332)
*Loriano Bonora*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出，Goldhaber等人1957年实验所发现的左手性无质量电子中微子，仅用Weyl旋量描述是不完整的，应采用由两个Weyl旋量构成的无质量Dirac旋量；进而提出左-右对称标准模型扩展，以解释其右手对应态为何不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修正标准模型中对中微子的Weyl旋量描述，使其符合量子场论基本要求，并解释右手电子中微子缺失问题。

Method: 理论分析与模型拓展：基于量子场论原则，论证无质量中微子需用Dirac旋量描述，并引入左-右对称的标准模型扩展。

Result: 指出Weyl旋量描述不完整，确立需用无质量Dirac旋量；提出左-右对称模型作为右手电子中微子不可见的可能机制。

Conclusion: Goldhaber实验中的中微子应由Dirac旋量描述，其右手分量虽存在但因左-右对称模型机制而不可观测。

Abstract: This comment is a comment stimulated by the 1957 Goldhaber et al. experiment, that detected a massless left-handed electron neutrino with negative helicity. The standard description of this neutrino is by means of a Weyl spinor. It is shown below that this description is incomplete, and in fact one needs a massless Dirac spinor to comply with the requirements of quantum field theory. A Dirac spinor is composed of two Weyl spinors. This in turn raises the necessity to explain the fate of the right-handed electron neutrino, partner of the Goldhaber et al. experiment's one. A possible explanation for its invisibility is suggested here in terms of a left-right symmetric extension of the standard model.

</details>


### [2] [Flavor Alignment and Operator Sensitivity in a Left--Right Universal-Seesaw Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31348)
*Claudio Coriano,Paul H. Frampton*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了一个具有轻子三重性约束的宇称相关双态普适跷跷板模型，研究了其味结构和低能算符敏感性，发现轻-轻中性流转换在特定基底下消失，但重质量仍非简并；通过与B介子衰变、K介子CP破坏等实验数据对比，指出K介子CP破坏是最灵敏的味探针，并讨论了重W_R玻色子真空层次微调问题及中微子分支的辐射张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超出标准模型的新物理如何解释味物理异常（如B介子稀有衰变、K介子CP破坏）并协调理论自然性（如真空层次问题）与实验约束。

Method: 构建含轻子三重性与宇称对称性的双态普适跷跷板模型，采用退化单态质量实现味对齐，计算中性流过程、B/K介子衰变、矢量共振产生率等可观测量，并与历史实验数据（如B_d,s→μμ、ε_K）进行数值比对。

Result: 发现K介子CP破坏对模型参数最敏感；低参考质量被历史数据排除；dimuon与辐射B衰变效应当前精度下难以分辨；W_R=80 TeV时真空层次需约5×10⁻⁶精细调节；Dirac型中微子分支存在额外辐射张力。

Conclusion: 该模型虽能实现味对齐并部分兼容现有数据，但面临K介子CP破坏强约束、精细调节问题及中微子热历史张力，需更高精度实验（如未来K介子实验）进一步检验。

Abstract: We analyse the flavour structure and low-energy operator sensitivity of a parity-related doublet universal-seesaw model with an imposed lepton triality. Degenerate bare singlet masses permit a basis in which light-light tree neutral flavour transitions vanish; the physical heavy masses remain nondegenerate. We connect this alignment property to the axial and scalar differences measured by $B_{d,s}^0\toμ^+μ^-$ and reproduce the six-state charged-vector mixing amplitudes for five reference masses. The reference analysis identifies kaon CP violation as the more sensitive flavour probe. Historical kaon data disfavour the lower reference masses, while estimates suggest that dimuon and radiative $B$-decay effects are harder to resolve at present precision. These comparisons use the specified reference spectrum and historical inputs. We also compare charged and neutral resonance rates with published search benchmarks. The vacuum hierarchy entails a cancellation of about $5\times10^{-6}$ at $M_{W_R}=80\,\mathrm{TeV}$. The initially thermal Dirac-neutrino branch has a dataset-dependent extra-radiation tension, strengthened in an illustrative mass-dependent decoupling estimate. For dimuon decays we establish operator selection rules and sensitivity estimates. The numerical flavour results cover meson mixing and charged-vector dipoles. We give no dimuon branching-fraction prediction or global allowed region.

</details>


### [3] [Superdaisy Resummation in Theories with Broken Gauge Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31359)
*Martin Münzenberg,Carlos Tamarit*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了有限温度有效势的部分dress resummation方案，应用于自发破缺规范理论的规范场和鬼场部分，并将其应用于标准模型。通过保留热自能的全质量依赖性并自洽求解标量、规范玻色子和鬼粒子的质量，发现超出O(T^2)近似的修正对规范玻色子热质量影响显著，而对标量谱影响较小；纵向规范玻色子的自洽dress降低了有限温度Higgs有效势的势垒，而横向部分的resummation则完全消除势垒，暗示一阶相变被排除，与格点结果一致；尽管横向激发强耦合导致该resummation定量不可靠，但结果凸显了横向模式在电弱相变性质中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高温近似（如O(T^2)）在处理电弱相变时存在局限，尤其难以准确刻画规范玻色子热质量及相变阶数；需发展更精确的有限温度有效势方法，涵盖全质量依赖性和自洽质量生成机制。

Method: 扩展部分dress resummation方案至规范与鬼场 sector；保留热自能的全质量依赖性；建立并求解标量、规范玻色子与鬼粒子质量的耦合gap方程；分别分析纵向与横向规范模式对Higgs有效势的影响。

Result: 规范玻色子热质量受高阶修正显著影响，而标量谱变化较小；纵向dress降低势垒，横向resummation完全消除势垒，暗示无第一阶电弱相变；横向模式虽因强耦合致定量不可靠，但其定性影响关键。

Conclusion: 横向规范模式在决定电弱相变性质中起决定性作用；当前resummation框架揭示了传统高温近似的不足，强调需更可靠方法（如格点或非微扰技术）处理强耦合横向激发。

Abstract: We extend the partial dressing resummation scheme for the finite-temperature effective potential to the gauge and ghost sectors of spontaneously broken gauge theories and apply the resulting framework to the Standard Model. Going beyond the usual high-temperature treatment, we retain the full mass dependence of the thermal self-energies and determine the scalar, gauge-boson and ghost masses self-consistently from a coupled system of gap equations. The solutions show that corrections beyond the leading $\mathcal{O}(T^2)$ approximation are particularly relevant for the gauge-boson thermal masses, while they induce a comparatively small shift in the scalar spectrum for the benchmark studied. The self-consistent gauge-boson dressing in the longitudinal sector reduces the barrier in the finite-temperature Higgs effective potential. Implementing the resummation in the transverse sector removes the barrier and would seem to exclude a first-order phase transition in accordance with lattice results. While such resummation is not quantitatively reliable, due to the transverse excitations becoming strongly coupled, the results showcase the crucial role played by transverse modes in determining the nature of the electroweak phase transition.

</details>


### [4] [$QQ\bar Q\bar Q$ Quark System and Gauge/String Duality II: String Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33140)
*Oleg Andreev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文是两篇配套论文的第二篇，利用规范/弦对偶方法构建全重四夸克系统，并将其扩展至包含两种等质量轻味的SU(3)规范理论；分析了矩形与共线构型下的最低两个Born-Oppenheimer势，揭示了与弦重连、断裂、顶点湮灭及收缩相关的关键尺度，并指出弦张力具有普适性而弦断裂距离则不然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 继续发展基于规范/弦对偶的全重四夸克系统构造，并推广到更现实的含轻味的SU(3)规范理论情形。

Method: 基于规范/弦对偶，将先前针对全重系统的构造推广至含两种等质量轻味的SU(3)规范理论；对矩形和共线构型下的最低两个Born-Oppenheimer势进行解析与数值分析。

Result: 识别出与弦重连、断裂、顶点湮灭和收缩相关的多个关键尺度；发现弦张力具有普适性，而弦断裂距离不具普适性。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了规范/弦对偶在含轻味强耦合系统中的应用，为理解四夸克态及弦动力学提供了新的定量见解。

Abstract: This is the second of two companion papers in which we continue developing the construction of the fully heavy tetraquark system using the gauge/string duality. Here, we extend this construction to model $SU(3)$ gauge theory with two light flavors of equal mass. As an application, we analyze the two lowest Born-Oppenheimer potentials, both analytically and numerically, for rectangular and collinear geometries. Our analysis reveals several critical scales associated with string reconnection and breaking, as well as junction annihilation and pinching. In addition, we find that the string tension is universal, whereas the string breaking distance is not.

</details>

*Tomohiro Abe,Ryosuke Sato,Ozan Semin,Takumu Yamanaka*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对G-宇称奇暗π介子暗物质的研究未考虑ρ介子的影响，而ρ介子作为矢量介子可能通过共振增强显著改变遗迹丰度，并因电弱相互作用导致与标准模型粒子混合，需系统评估其影响及实验可探测性。

Method: 在SU(N_c)类QCD暗强相互作用框架下，引入暗ρ矢量介子，计算其与暗π介子的耦合、与标准模型规范玻色子的混合效应；结合热退耦机制计算遗迹丰度；利用ATLAS/CMS现有搜寻结果进行限制，并模拟FCC-hh与μ子对撞机中e⁺e⁻、μ⁺μ⁻、W⁺W⁻等共振末态的信号截面。

Result: ρ介子的共振增强可大幅改变暗物质遗迹丰度预测；电弱混合开启新衰变道并修正暗π有效相互作用；ATLAS/CMS数据已对部分参数空间给出约束；FCC-hh有望探测质量达40 TeV的暗ρ介子。

Conclusion: 暗强相互作用理论中ρ介子不可忽略，其共振与混合效应深刻影响暗物质唯象学；未来高能对撞机特别是FCC-hh具备强大探测潜力，为该类模型提供关键检验平台。

Abstract: We extend previous studies of $\mathcal{G}$-parity-odd dark pion dark matter in a QCD-like $\mathrm{SU}(N_c)$ gauge theory with electroweakly interacting dark quarks to include the effects of $ρ$ mesons. We show that vector mesons can substantially affect relic abundance calculations through resonant enhancement, significantly affecting the conditions required to reproduce the observed relic abundance. Electroweak interactions further induce mixing between the dark $ρ$ mesons and Standard Model gauge bosons, opening new decay channels for the dark $ρ$ and modifying the effective interactions of the dark pions. We recast existing ATLAS and CMS searches to constrain the model, and perform a collider phenomenology study targeting the future FCC-hh and muon colliders, focusing on the resonant enhancement of cross sections in the $e^+e^-$, $μ^+μ^-$, and $W^+W^-$ final states for the latter. We show that the FCC-hh could probe $ρ$ mesons whose mass is up to 40 TeV.

</details>


### [6] [Discovering Warped Extra Dimensions at Next Generation Dark Matter Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31373)
*Kaustubh Agashe,Abhishek Banerjee,Kushan Panchal,Nicholas L. Rodd*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了来自弯曲额外维度的暗物质（DM）模型，指出其在直接和间接探测实验中仍具可发现性；提出Z'型门户机制，并讨论质量调谐、GUT紫外完备化及超对称增强方案，最终强调CTAO等设施有望在近期发现此类信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着WIMP暗物质探测进入中微子背景极限，需探索新理论框架（如弯曲额外维度）以解释暗物质并协调实验可探测性与理论自然性（如等级问题）。

Method: 基于弯曲额外维模型构建暗物质场景，分析Z'门户耦合特性、质量调谐要求；引入大统一理论（GUT）实现紫外完备化，并结合超对称机制缓解小等级问题；评估直接与间接探测信号强度。

Result: 发现TeV量级弱耦合DM与10 TeV强耦合Z'门户均具实验可行性；提出一种抑制直接探测但保留强间接信号的变体模型；加入超对称后获得TeV质量弱耦合Z'中介子，提升自然性；CTAO等设施有望近期发现信号。

Conclusion: 弯曲额外维框架下的暗物质模型兼具理论动机与实验可检验性，其首个实验证据可能来自高能宇宙线观测而非地下探测，为探索额外维度提供新路径。

Abstract: The coming years will see what could be the final push in the search for WIMP dark matter (DM), with direct detection reaching into the neutrino fog and indirect detection reaching TeV-scale thermal relics. We study a class of models that these experiments could discover: DM emerging from a warped extra dimension. In the IR, these scenarios appear generically as a $Z'$-type portal. We show that DM with electroweak couplings and TeV scale masses remains viable and discoverable within such a bottom-up context. However, the models require a 20\% tuning between the DM and $Z'$ masses, a tuning that is relaxed if the DM and $Z'$ are strongly coupled and heavier ($\sim$$10$ TeV). In fact, it is the strongly-coupled $Z'$-type portal which is UV-completed by the framework of a warped extra-dimensional Grand Unified Theory (GUT), where DM stability is a spin-off of suppressing proton decay, resulting in a $\sim$$10$ TeV Kaluza-Klein (KK) scale. We identify a variant of this framework where all gauge fields except a baryon-number gauge boson have suppressed couplings to the DM; this model significantly suppresses the direct detection rate, but it remains discoverable through indirect detection. Further, to address the little hierarchy problem associated with $\sim$$10$ TeV KK scale, inspired by the Super-Composite Higgs, we augment our extra-dimensional GUT setup with supersymmetry. We show that this model also possesses a DM candidate, now with a weakly-coupled and $\sim$${\rm TeV}$ mass $Z'$ mediator. In all cases, a near-term discovery remains possible, for instance with the now online Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO), suggesting that our first hint of extra dimensions could emerge from the high-energy sky.

</details>

*Xiaolin Ma,Volodymyr Takhistov,Hideo Iizuka*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Casimir实验仅限于约束自旋无关的短程力，而对自旋相关的轴子介导力缺乏有效探测手段。

Method: 利用磁补偿源在不同自旋极化方向下的测量，使自旋相关的轴子力信号反号，同时使Casimir力、引力和静电力等背景噪声大幅抵消。

Result: 该方法可在微米级间距下无需屏蔽层进行大尺寸探针测量，并在1–100微米作用距离上将轴子耦合常数g_p^e g_s^N和g_p^e g_s^e的实验灵敏度提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: Casimir实验可拓展为高灵敏度自旋依赖短程力探测平台，显著超越当前实验室对轴子类粒子的限制。

Abstract: Precision Casimir experiments enable highly sensitive probes of short range forces, but have thus far constrained only spin-independent interactions. We show that measurements between reversed polarizations of a magnetically compensated source enable direct searches of axion-mediated monopole-dipole forces. The spin force reverses sign, while the common Casimir, gravitational and electrostatic backgrounds broadly cancel. Compensated sources need no intervening shielding, allowing large probes at micrometer gaps. Casimir experiments could probe the couplings $g_p^e g_s^{N}$ and $g_p^e g_s^{e}$ at $λ\sim1-100~μ{\rm m}$ orders of magnitude beyond existing laboratory searches.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为探索轻子味破坏的新物理信号，特别是解释CMS实验中 eμ 共振超出（m_X≈146 GeV），本文构建一个实中性标量X模型，仅含 e-μ 耦合，以在高精度正负电子对撞机（FCC-ee）上开展探测。

Result: 在 m_X = 146 GeV 处，电荷平衡通道对 |h| 的95% CL 预期灵敏度约为 1.0×10⁻²（含1%背景不确定性），而同号通道达 1.7×10⁻³；后者优于当前铯基电子g-2实验给出的上限 |h| < 1.46×10⁻²，表明该通道可突破现有约束。两信号事例率本征相等，灵敏度差异源于背景水平悬殊。

Abstract: We study a real neutral scalar $X$ with a single flavour-off-diagonal coupling $h_{eμ}=h_{μe}\equiv h$. The coupling induces associated production, $e^+e^-\to e^\pmμ^\mp X$, and the decay $X\to e^\pmμ^\mp$. The resulting four-lepton final state separates into the charge-balanced channel $e^+e^-μ^+μ^-$ and the same-sign same-flavour channels $e^\pm e^\pmμ^\mpμ^\mp$. The charge-balanced channel receives a large prompt continuum background, while the same-sign category has a small estimated background under our benchmark assumptions. We analyse the two channels at FCC-ee for $\sqrt{s}=240~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $\mathcal{L}=10.8~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, including a detector simulation. The collider sensitivities are compared with the one-coupling constraints from the electron and muon magnetic moments, muonium--antimuonium conversion, and LEP dilepton data. At $m_X=146~\mathrm{GeV}$, motivated by the largest excess in the CMS $eμ$ resonance search, the expected 95\% CL reach of the charge-balanced channel, including a 1\% background uncertainty, is $|h|\simeq 1.0 \times 10^{-2}$, while the same-sign channel reaches $|h|\simeq 1.7 \times 10^{-3}$, compared with the cesium-based electron-$g-2$ bound $|h|<1.46\times10^{-2}$. Both channels thus probe couplings below the present bound. The two signal rates are equal before selection; the difference in reach follows from the different backgrounds.

</details>

The models and the inputs of our results are available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24345218. We translate the latest LHC searches at 13 and 13.6 TeV into the parameter space of the model. For example, at our benchmark points, with partners near their present limits and an electroweak triplet at about 4 TeV, 73-83% of the triplet decays contain partners, and vector-mediated production reaches up to three times the QCD rate. At the HL-LHC this region can be probed by resonance searches extended to large widths and by partner-pair searches that include resonant production; the latter also reveal the partner spectrum. Abstract: Foundation models are a new approach to building machine learning algorithms for particle physics. Their central idea is that a single model can be adapted to a variety of downstream tasks, amortizing training costs. So far, most foundation model prototypes have operated on the constituents of a single jet. This limits their usefulness for physics problems at the scale of full events, such as distinguishing different processes, and restricts their performance, as global correla- tions are invisible to the model. Here, we introduce OmniX, an architecture that operates on both single jets and whole events within a single model. Using the jet-level JetClass and Top-tagging datasets and the event-level Collide-2V dataset, we demonstrate that OmniX trained jointly on single jets and full events matches or exceeds otherwise equivalent jet-only and event-only models in generation and classification.

Abstract: In warm inflation with the Standard Model (SM), an axion-like inflaton dissipates energy into the plasma through QCD sphaleron transitions. Current predictions assume a friction linear in the bias, defined as the free energy released per transition in units of the temperature. The friction, however, has only been simulated at moderate biases in pure SU(2) gauge theory. We show that, during slow roll, energy conservation, the axial anomaly and thermodynamics relate the bias to the quark chemical potentials generated by the transitions, independently of the form of the friction and of the reaction network in the plasma. A bias within the simulated range requires chemical potentials a few times the temperature, which typically lead to Higgs condensation. Conversely, small chemical potentials imply a large bias and, unless the dissipation is very weak, a temperature far above the axion decay constant, which requires a cutoff of the effective description above that temperature. We illustrate that deviations from linearity beyond the simulated range can change the inflationary dynamics qualitatively, so that reliable predictions require a real-time computation of the friction and the noise in QCD at large bias. With high-scale lepton-number violation, the quark chemical potentials also source a $B-L$ asymmetry that exceeds the observed value by many orders of magnitude already during inflation. Finally, we show that the same relation between the bias and the chemical potentials holds for derivative couplings of the inflaton to SM currents. Unless the dissipation is very weak, warm inflation driven by such couplings therefore requires either a temperature far above the decay constant or chemical potentials that typically lead to Higgs condensation.

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the multi-component axion dark matter (DM) scenario and explore the role of ultralight axion-like particles (ALPs). Abstract: The construction of canonical basis is an important procedure in the calculation of multi-loop Feynman integrals via the canonical differential equation methods. Currently, there are various different methods for this purpose. Among them, no single method is universally applicable. Thus, obtaining a valid basis often requires repeated attempts and verification. In this paper, we present CANON, an agent-assisted framework designed to make this search-and-verification process more convenient and more efficient. In this workflow, the agent selects methods and calls the corresponding tools to revise the basis proposal or the associated differential-equation system. Independent programs then verify whether the agent's result is valid and ask the agent to retry if verification fails. This framework contains a specific state-management tool to supports long-range basis searching works which requires multiple sub-agents. Its modular architecture enables future extensions to the framework itself. In this paper, we demonstrate this framework by applying it to some two-loop and three-loop integral families.

Abstract: We study the classical limit of two-to-two massive-scalar amplitudes at all orders of perturbation theory. We compute these amplitudes in the probe limit for several theories: a family of scalar theories, electrodynamics, and $\mathcal{N}=8$ supergravity with half-BPS matter. We show that related multiple-emission classical amplitudes obey a simple factorization derivable from the usual factorization of quantum amplitudes. The classical factorization, which reflects the second-order nature of classical dynamics, holds in more general theories --- including gravity. We further show that both the multiple-emission and the two-to-two classical amplitudes have a simple organization in terms of weighted trees. The expansion only requires a separation of scales and the adiabaticity of the heavy mode, while the reflection symmetry holds for many systems in de Sitter space. Abstract: We review the chiral anomaly from a historical perspective, from the $π^0\toγγ$ puzzle and the work of Adler, Bell and Jackiw, to the Wess-Zumino-Witten (WZW) term. We then describe how the WZW term was motivated from quantum chromodynamics in 1983-85. Integrating out the non-Abelian gauge fields, that have a gapped spectrum, gives a Nambu-Jona-Lasinio type theory of quarks, and integrating out the quarks, which are gapped by chiral symmetry breaking, gives the WZW model. The fermion determinant splits into a regulator-dependent modulus, which gives the dynamics of mesons, and a universal phase, which is fixed by the anomaly and is the WZW term. Restricted to the collective coordinates of the baryon soliton, the WZW term imposes a U(1) constraint that makes the baryon wave functions monopole harmonics and fixes their spin, statistics and flavour multiplets. We then explain that this phase is a Berry phase: the Schwinger terms of current algebra are the curvature of a U(1) connection on the space of chiral field configurations, and the WZW term is its holonomy, which we call a Berry-Wilson loop. In 1+1 dimensions we write the connection explicitly in terms of Euler angles, exhibiting a Dirac monopole whose quantised charge is the level of the current algebra.

Abstract: Radiation transport is an essential process in many physical simulations, including cosmological codes which track stellar and diffuse radiation. Many present codes utilize ray tracing or moment methods, but these grow increasingly difficult and expensive for large simulations. Quantum lattice Boltzmann methods exponentially reduce complexity by propagating radiation over a lattice, allowing for better spatial resolution without needing to introduce adaptive ray tracing or other optimizations. However, existing formulations result in poor angular resolution for radiation point sources. In this work, we propose an angular redistribution methodology which significantly improves the resolution with minimal increase in complexity. We verify the correctness of the method on both classical processors and quantum simulators and outline how the quantum algorithm can be integrated into existing codes. This method is an important step towards fully-quantum radiation hydrodynamics simulation which could exponentially accelerate existing cosmological codes, enabling simulations at scales currently infeasible.

It provides a unified framework for fitting relative astrometry, radial velocities, and absolute astrometry, supporting analyses of both resolved and unresolved systems. Available posterior sampling methods include parallel-tempered Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) through ptemcee (Foreman-Mackey et al., 2013; Vousden et al., 2016) and nested sampling through dynesty (Speagle, 2020), nautilus (Lange, 2023), and pymultinest (Buchner et al., 2014; Feroz et al., 2019). This paper accompanies the release of orbitize! version 4.0 and describes the functionality added since version 3.0 (Blunt et al., 2024), with a particular focus on joint orbit fitting with Gaia DR4 epoch astrometry and the implementation of the Orbits for the Extremely Impatient (OFTeI) algorithm (Blunt et al. 2026b, in preparation).

Abstract: The area common to three disks determines how much light is blocked during mutual events of transiting planets, moons, and eclipsing multiple stars. We present a new solution that removes every case distinction from the evaluation and corrects several errors in earlier work. We prove that a non-degenerate overlap has at most four corners, which reduces the geometry to five topologies that a single directed walk along the boundary arcs resolves. Green's theorem then yields the area as a sum of circular segments and a vertex polygon. The evaluation needs one arctangent per arc, suffers no subtractive cancellation, and handles arcs longer than $π$ without a special case. We verify the solution for all cases and show that the algorithm is componentwise backward stable. Five public packages that compute the same area fail on some of our test configurations, either through undefined values, missing topologies, or lost precision. Abstract: Thermal noise limits the sensitivity of precision mechanical instruments. We present a thermal- noise formulation for lumped-element mechanical systems that writes the total displacement thermal noise at the readout as the sum of contributions from all dissipative elements. For each contribution, we separate the thermal force generated by the element from its transfer to the readout. This decomposition provides simple design rules for comparing contributions from elements in series and in parallel and identifying the dominant noise source. It applies to frequency-dependent dissipation and to elements at different steady temperatures under local thermal equilibrium. We use it to identify the frequency regimes in which parallel and series dissipation dilution reduce displacement thermal noise. We then use examples of a pendulum on a lossy support, a rotational flexure, and rigidly mounted lunar optics to show how support noise can dominate even when the support has little effect on the system dynamics. Abstract: We present details of a new two-element radio interferometer, namely the TWIN, developed for routine monitoring of solar events at the Gauribidanur Observatory (77.428°E, 13.603°N) between 185 MHz and 350 MHz. The oscilloscope digitises the RF signals with a time resolution of 0.8 ns, bursts of 9952 time samples, with a nominal duty cycle of 3 seconds, and the resultant data is collected on a remote computer. Specialised tools were developed to analyse the data and detect solar events. The interferometer has been operational since July 2023. In our analysis, we have detected several solar events from the ongoing Solar Cycle 25. This paper outlines the design philosophy, signal flow, commissioning and validation, results from a solar event, and future plans for data capture, data analysis, and archival.

Abstract: The scientific efficiency of ground-based astronomical observatories relies on effective decision-making in a dynamic, multi-timescale environment with complex scientific rewards. Traditional observation schedulers are designed and tuned by domain experts to handle these considerations using heuristic rules based on models of the environment and observational system. Here, we leverage historical data to train an automated telescope scheduler using behavior cloning (BC). The BC scheduler is trained purely on historical datasets; it is not given expert scheduling heuristics nor an optimal scientific reward. It is then evaluated in order of increasing realism: first on expert states, then in closed-loop offline rollouts, and finally during autonomous operation with real-time feedback on the NSF's Victor M.\ Blanco 4-m Telescope. On expert states from holdout nights, the BC policy closely matches the expert distribution over airmass and Moon separation. During closed-loop rollouts on the same nights, we find that the per-observation data-quality metric, $t_{\rm eff}$, falls by $6.9 \pm 3.4\%$ relative to the expert's. During on-sky deployment, the policy scheduled $149$ exposures in $5.2$ hours with an average $t_{\rm eff} = 0.75 \pm 0.26$, consistent with closed-loop rollout evaluations. No additional degradation was observed when transitioning to real observing conditions. This represents the first real-world, real-time deployment of a learned autonomous scheduler on a leadership-class national observatory and establishes a path toward on-sky evaluation of reinforcement-learning-based schedulers trained on archival schedules augmented with counterfactual trajectories.

</details>

Abstract: We present Hesperos & Eosphoros, a forced-photometry operator and a forward model that, respectively, perform and model the spectrophotometric measurement of the Spectro-Photometer for the History of the Universe, Epoch of Reionization, and Ices Explorer (SPHEREx). ### [88] [In-in formalism & $\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-product: no contribution from total time derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31272)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清文献中关于总时间导数在in-in形式论中是否在评估点（暴胀结束时刻）产生非平凡贡献的争议。

Result: 证明评估点（即in-in路径的转向点）在$\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-ordering下仅为中间点，所有看似在该点出现的边界贡献（尤其是此前被忽略的与$\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-ordering相关的接触项）严格相消。

Conclusion: 总时间导数在in-in形式论中对暴胀关联函数在评估点不产生非平凡贡献；此前声称存在此类贡献的观点源于对算符序和路径积分轮廓的误解。

Abstract: In the in-in formalism, total time derivatives are sometimes argued to contribute nontrivially to inflationary correlators at the evaluation point, i.e., at the end of inflation. In this paper, we disprove this claim based on the path integral. After formulating the canonical quantization and the regulated path integral of systems with total time derivatives, we derive the $\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-ordering, the correct operator ordering on the closed time path. In the $\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-ordering, the evaluation point, or the turning point of the in-in contour, is merely an intermediate point and is never an endpoint of the generalized path integral contour. As a result, any apparent boundary contributions at the turning point of the forward and backward branches completely cancel out. We demonstrate this cancellation by re-analyzing examples in the literature, with an emphasis on contact terms associated with the $\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-ordering overlooked previously.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用长伽马暴X射线平台期发射，通过二维Dainotti关系标准化；构建两个样本：铂金样本（P50，50个GRB，基于光变质量筛选）与磁星样本（M23，23个GRB，要求磁星自转模型统计上优于Willingale参数化）；分别以Ia型超新星和宇宙时钟为锚点校准Dainotti关系，并用MCMC在平直ΛCDM模型中联合推断；另提出一种同步拟合单个平台光变与Dainotti关系的新型联合似然方法。

Result: M23样本本征散射比P50低约41%；联合SNe Ia与CC后，两样本给出高度一致的宇宙学参数：H₀=69.45±0.32（M23）与69.46±0.33 km/s/Mpc（P50），Ωₘ=0.34^{+0.01}_{-0.02}（M23）与0.34±0.02（P50），均与Planck和SH0ES结果存在3–3.5σ张力；新联合似然方法使本征散射相较P50再降约56%。

Conclusion: 磁星模型筛选的GRB平台期是稳健、高红移的距离阶梯补充探针，具备随样本增大而提升约束效率的明确路径。

Abstract: The persistent tension in Hubble constant measurements and recent DESI hints of dynamical dark energy motivate extending the distance ladder beyond Type~Ia supernovae (SNe~Ia) and Cosmic Chronometers (CC) into the largely unexplored range $z\sim2$--$6$. We use X-ray plateau emission of long gamma-ray bursts (GRBs), standardized through the two-dimensional Dainotti relation, as a probe of this gap. We analyze two samples: a platinum sample (P50, 50~GRBs, light-curve quality cuts) and a magnetar sample (M23, 23~GRBs, selected by requiring the magnetar spin-down model to be statistically preferred over the Willingale parametrization). We calibrate the Dainotti relation anchored independently to the Master SNe~Ia compilation and to Cosmic Chronometers, and propagate the result into a joint flat-$Λ$CDM inference via MCMC. Despite containing less than half the GRBs of P50, M23 yields an intrinsic scatter $\sim41\%$ smaller, demonstrating that physically motivated selection outperforms quality cuts. Both samples give nearly identical cosmological constraints once combined with SNe~Ia and CC: M23 gives $H_0=69.45\pm0.32\ \mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ and $Ω_m=0.34^{+0.01}_{-0.02}$; P50 gives $H_0=69.46\pm0.33$ and $Ω_m=0.34\pm0.02$, both in $3$--$3.5σ$ tension with Planck and SH0ES. We further introduce a novel joint likelihood that simultaneously fits individual plateau light curves and the Dainotti correlation, reducing the intrinsic scatter by $\sim56\%$ relative to the platinum sample and avoiding local maxima from sparse light-curve data. These results establish magnetar-selected GRB plateaus as a robust, high-redshift complement to existing distance-ladder probes, with a clear path toward improved constraining efficiency as sample sizes grow.

</details>


### [90] [CosmoMap: Predicting Hot Gas Temperature and Density from Dark Matter with a Conditioned 3D U-Net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31282)
*Aman Bhutani,Maggie Lieu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CosmoMap的3D U-Net模型，利用暗物质密度和速度场，结合宇宙学与反馈参数条件，高效预测星系团、星系群及宇宙网中热气体的温度和电子密度，显著优于传统机器学习方法，并在多红移、多种宇宙学模型下保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线模拟需要大体积、多宇宙学下的热气体温密信息，而流体动力学模拟计算代价高昂，亟需快速、准确的替代模型。

Method: 提出CosmoMap：一个FiLM条件化的3D U-Net模型，以暗物质密度和速度为输入，以宇宙学参数（如Ω_m, σ_8）和反馈参数为条件，输出气体温度T和电子密度n_e；在CAMELS-IllustrisTNG拉丁超立方体数据集上训练和评估。

Conclusion: CosmoMap是高效、泛化性强的热气体物理代理模型，适用于大规模X射线模拟和宇宙学分析，但在致密结构分辨率和最亮气体密度预测上仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Mock X-ray observations of hot gas in galaxy groups, clusters and the cosmic web require its temperature and density over large volumes and many cosmologies, for which hydrodynamical simulations are expensive. We present CosmoMap, a 3D U-Net FiLM-conditioned on cosmological and feedback parameters that predicts gas temperature $T$ and electron density $n_{\rm e}$ from dark matter density and velocity. On the CAMELS IllustrisTNG Latin hypercube, CosmoMap reaches $R^2 = 0.765$ for $\log T$ and $0.934$ for $\log n_{\rm e}$ at $z=0$, reducing the error of gradient-boosted trees by 22-23 per cent. Temperature is predicted best in the densest regions, mostly galaxy- and group-scale haloes ($R^2 = 0.88$), but the electron density of the brightest X-ray-emitting gas is biased low by $0.12$ dex. One model outperforms the baselines at every redshift up to $z=2$, and using inputs from gravity-only simulations costs little accuracy. The predictions reproduce scaling relations, surface brightness profile shapes and most of the cosmological information in the gas. They produce box-integrated luminosities with a median bias of $-0.16$ dex, sensitive at the $0.2$ dex level to the inference scheme unless batch normalisation is used. Training for per-voxel uncertainties with a heteroscedastic likelihood costs accuracy and lowers luminosities by a further $0.1$-$0.3$ dex; the composite-loss variant, CosmoMap-$σ$, provides uncertainties that are calibrated at 68 per cent after rescaling by predicted emissivity, except for the density of the brightest gas. Compact structures are smoothed: mock X-ray lightcones recover 89 per cent of the brightest tenth of sources but 28 per cent of all sources.

</details>


### [91] [Enhanced spatial resolution and performance for N-body simulation of self-interacting dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31364)
*Moritz S. Fischer*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的数值方案来模拟自相互作用暗物质（SIDM），通过设定依赖于相对速度和时间步长的距离阈值，使粒子对在该阈值内始终发生相互作用，从而提升空间分辨率、降低计算成本并提高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有N体模拟在处理自相互作用暗物质时难以分辨引力热塌缩晕中的平均自由程，需增加分辨率单元数，导致计算开销大；亟需一种不增加粒子数却能提升分辨率和效率的新方法。

Method: 摒弃传统基于核函数与相互作用概率的计算方式，采用距离阈值判据：当两粒子间距小于依赖其相对速度和时间步长的动态阈值时，强制发生相互作用。

Result: 新方案显著降低计算成本，SIDM自相互作用部分仅占总计算量的百分之几；在多个基准测试中验证了其高精度，并在模拟引力热塌缩SIDM晕时实现了更高空间分辨率和准确性。

Conclusion: 该新数值方案优于现有SIDM模拟方法，既提升了计算性能，又增强了对关键天体物理过程（如 gravothermal collapse）的模拟能力，有助于解决当前SIDM研究中的核心挑战。

Abstract: Studying the implications of self-interacting dark matter (SIDM) on astrophysical objects often requires simulations. N-body simulations are a standard tool but face a few challenges. One is resolving the mean free path in gravothermally collapsing halos. This is necessary to obtain accurate results and is achieved in state-of-the-art simulations by increasing the number of resolution elements. I aim to formulate a numerical scheme that gives an enhanced spatial resolution compared to those methods and allows increasing the resolution without changing the number of resolution elements. The novel scheme that I present is formulated by discarding the classic approach of using kernel functions and computing interaction probabilities. Instead, pairs of particles always interact if their distance is below a specific threshold. Importantly, this distance threshold depends on their relative velocity and the size of the time step. This new formulation no longer requires some of the computations in state-of-the-art schemes and thus helps to increase the performance. Through a variety of test problems with known solutions, I study the accuracy of the new scheme and demonstrate its advantages over state-of-the-art SIDM formulations. Notably, the scheme reduced the computational costs, making the SIDM computations typically much cheaper than gravity. As a consequence, a typical SIDM simulation of isotropic scattering requires only a few percent of its total computational cost for the self-interactions. In conclusion, the new scheme presents an improvement over previous schemes and helps to address current challenges. Besides the enhanced performance, it allows us to achieve a higher accuracy when simulating gravothermally collapsing SIDM halos.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决暗能量难题，探索宇宙加速是否可由非均匀结构形成的广义相对论效应（而非引入宇宙学常数或新物理）自然解释。

Method: 构建一个能精确满足爱因斯坦场方程的新解，其在早期恢复FLRW对称性，在晚期体现非均匀性；利用DESI的BAO数据和Ia型超新星数据反演Misner-Sharp质量，并结合其大尺度渐近行为推导有效宇宙学常数。

Result: 首次获得MS质量的观测约束；导出的宇宙学常数值与ΛCDM高度一致；表明非线性结构的GR效应可在不引入暗能量的情况下再现加速膨胀。

Conclusion: 宇宙加速可能源于大尺度结构的广义相对论非线性效应，MS质量的观测约束支持这一替代理论；未来可通过CMB透镜和晚ISW效应等多探针观测进一步检验。

Abstract: We present the first observational constraints on the Misner-Sharp (MS) mass of the universe, within a newly proposed exact General Relativity solution providing a non-perturbative description of nonlinear cosmic structure formation. At early times, when statistical homogeneity and isotropy are satisfied, our model reduces to usual FLRW cosmology, accommodating Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) data. At late times, the departure from homogeneity and isotropy on scales $\lesssim 200$ Mpc -- codified in the scale-dependent redshift distribution of the MS mass -- naturally induces cosmic acceleration, through an extensive term generated by the MS mass asymptotic limit on large scales. We derive the MS mass from Baryon Acoustic Oscillation data from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) and Type Ia Supernovae, obtaining a value of the cosmological constant in agreement with $Λ$CDM results. We outline broader implications and future observational prospects, highlighting how multi-probe strategies -- particularly CMB lensing and the late Integrated Sachs-Wolfe effect -- can ultimately test this solution to the dark energy puzzle.

</details>


### [93] [Parallel and perpendicular: a two-population test with POSSUM radio galaxies confirms hint of CMB birefringence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31411)
*Marcus Brüggen,Jorinde Dreismann,Pranjal Trivedi,Shane P. O'Sullivan,Takuya Akahori,Fumihiro Naokawa,Cameron L. Van Eck,Marco Regis*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过分析射电星系的偏振角与位置角的关系，独立验证了宇宙微波背景（CMB）中观测到的宇宙双折射效应（cosmic birefringence），测得旋转角β=0.282°±0.056°（5.0σ显著性），支持CMB结果，并暗示可能存在晚期演化的轴子类暗能量场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMB极化分析发现约0.2°–0.3°的各向同性偏振面旋转（宇宙双折射），暗示宇称破缺物理；但需在低红移、不同系统误差下进行独立检验。射电星系因其法拉第去旋转偏振角χ₀与位置角ψ存在统计对齐，可提供该检验。

Method: 引入χ₀−ψ≈0°和≈90°两类源（此前仅用后者），采用两个轴向von-Mises分布加均匀背景建模，使用包含各源角度误差的无 bins 似然函数；应用于POSSUM巡天中33,298个高信噪比、间距超半束宽的双源组分。

Result: 测得CMB惯例下的β=0.48°±0.35°，与CMB结果在符号与幅度上一致；加入0°群体使β方差减半；排除束内成对源后结果稳健；联合ACT+Planck晚期旋转似然得β=0.282°±0.056°（5.0σ）。

Conclusion: z∼0.5处射电星系证实了近5 Gyr以来存在与CMB量级相当的宇宙双折射，倾向晚期演化场（如轴子类暗能量）而非早期暗物质主导模型；完整POSSUM巡天有望在绝对角度定标下独立检验CMB结果。

Abstract: Analyses of the cosmic microwave background (CMB) polarisation find an isotropic rotation of the linear polarisation plane, cosmic birefringence, of $β\sim0.2^\circ$--$0.3^\circ$, hinting at parity-violating physics. An independent test at low redshift with different systematics is needed. Radio galaxies provide such a test because their Faraday de-rotated polarisation angle $χ_0$ is statistically aligned with their position angle $ψ$. Previous tests fitted only the population with $χ_0-ψ\approx90^\circ$. We add the population with $χ_0-ψ\approx0^\circ$, which is rotated by the same angle. We fit $χ_0-ψ$ with two axial von-Mises distributions plus a uniform background, with an unbinned likelihood including each source's angle error. We apply this to 33,298 components of 16,649 double radio sources from the Polarisation Sky Survey of the Universe's Magnetism (POSSUM), with both components at polarised S/N $>10$ and separated by more than half the beam. Both populations are equally abundant. In the CMB convention we measure $β=0.48^\circ\pm0.35^\circ$, agreeing with the CMB in sign and amplitude. Including the $0^\circ$ population halves the variance of $β$. Components closer than half a beam carry no information on the source axis; excluding them leaves $β$ unchanged and makes the two populations consistent in all morphological classes. Combining with the joint ACT and Planck likelihood for a late-time rotation yields $β=0.282^\circ\pm0.056^\circ$, $5.0σ$ from zero. At $z\sim0.5$, radio galaxies probe the rotation of the last $\sim$ 5 Gyr. A CMB-sized rotation there would point to a late-evolving field such as axion-like dark energy, whereas a rotation before reionisation, e.g. by axion-like dark matter, predicts $β\approx0$. Given absolute angle calibration, the full POSSUM survey can test the CMB result independently.

</details>


### [94] [Symbolic Regression Diagnostics of 1D Lyman Alpha Forest Emulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31450)
*Aidan Behmer,Ming-Feng Ho,Camille Avestruz,Yanhui Yang,Simeon Bird*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文使用符号回归诊断黑箱多保真度模拟器（用于Lyman-alpha森林1D通量功率谱）的参数依赖性，提出导数保真诊断和Sobolev损失以提升符号方程对参数梯度的准确性，使其适用于Fisher预测等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归可简化复杂参数空间、节省计算资源并揭示参数间依赖关系，但其生成的方程虽能较好拟合 emulator 输出，却常无法准确复现参数导数，限制其在 Fisher 预报等梯度敏感任务中的应用。

Method: 基于预训练的高斯过程（GP）多保真度 emulator，采用符号回归（PySR）搜索显式解析表达式来刻画功率谱对分辨率及各宇宙学/天体物理参数的依赖；引入导数保真诊断，并设计 Sobolev 损失（联合拟合函数值与梯度）优化符号方程。

Result: 在 z=3.6 处，Sobolev 损失使 11 个 PRIYA 参数中 9 个的符号方程达到导数保真；对参数 h 和 AGN 效果不佳（因其功率谱印记弱或存在简并）；相比增加模型复杂度，重定义训练目标更高效鲁棒。

Conclusion: 符号回归可作为 emulator 的有效诊断工具，尤其适用于低维或弱耦合参数依赖场景；本文方法缓解了 PySR 在梯度敏感任务中的失效问题，为未来将符号回归与 emulator 结合提供了实践路径与局限警示。

Abstract: We use symbolic regression to diagnose the parameter dependence of a black-box multi-fidelity emulator of the Lyman-alpha forest 1D flux power spectrum. Symbolic regression is appealing in its ability to reduce complex parameter spaces, thereby reducing compute time and offering potential interpretability in parameter inter-dependencies. Starting from a pretrained Gaussian-process (GP) emulator that connects low- and high-resolution PRIYA simulations, we search for explicit equations describing the dependence of the power spectrum on resolution and each cosmological and astrophysical parameter. We find that symbolic regression can recover equations that reproduce the emulator values well, but accurate predictions do not guarantee accurate parameter derivatives, making such equations unreliable for downstream applications such as Fisher forecasts. We therefore introduce a derivative-faithfulness diagnostic that directly compares the parameter gradient of each symbolic equation to the GP, and explore a derivative-matching (Sobolev) loss to improve this agreement. At z = 3.6, the Sobolev objective yields derivative-faithful equations for 9 of the 11 PRIYA parameters constraining the Lyman-alpha forest, with the approach only ineffective on h and AGN, whose 1D power spectrum imprints are weak or degenerate. Redefining the training objective with the Sobolev loss is a more robust and computationally efficient approach to gradient-matching repair than increasing model complexity. Our methods present potential mitigation strategies for failure modes of PySR, enabling downstream Fisher forecasts in some of these cases. Further, we document limitations potentially relevant to future work bridging symbolic regression with emulators. Our results demonstrate symbolic regression as an emulator diagnostic tool, with particular utility for low-dimensional or weakly coupled parameter dependencies.

</details>


### [95] [Learning to find cosmic filaments with graph neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31494)
*Alexis Tastet,Daniela Galárraga-Espinosa,Leander Thiele*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流匹配和图神经网络的生成式方法，用于从星系巡天数据中推断宇宙大尺度结构（特别是宇宙细丝），并建模其不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙细丝构成复杂的网络结构，仅凭离散的星系样本难以无偏重建，需对重建不确定性进行建模。

Method: 采用基于流匹配（flow matching）的生成模型结合图神经网络（GNN），以星系分布为条件生成细丝结构的分布。

Result: 模型成功复现目标细丝分布的关键统计特性，并输出细丝构型的概率分布，验证了其不确定性建模能力。

Conclusion: 该方法是直接从星系巡天数据概率性推断宇宙网的初步尝试，也展示了条件生成模型在稀疏观测数据下重建类细丝结构的潜力。

Abstract: Inferring the large-scale structure of the Universe from galaxy surveys is a fundamental challenge in cosmology. In particular, cosmic filaments form an extended and geometrically complex network whose underlying structure cannot be reconstructed from discrete galaxy catalogs without uncertainty. We investigate a generative approach to filament finding based on flow matching and graph neural networks. The learned model reproduces key statistical properties of the target filament distribution while generating a distribution over possible filament configurations, demonstrating its ability to model uncertainty. Our work provides a first step towards probabilistic inference of the cosmic web directly from galaxy surveys. More broadly, these results demonstrate the potential of conditional generative models for reconstructing filament-like structures from sparse observational tracers.

</details>


### [96] [A unified stochastic random walk theory for Kolmogorov spectrum, halo mass functions, and halo density profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31746)
*Zhijie Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的随机框架来描述非平衡系统，将湍流和宇宙结构形成都建模为尺度空间中的随机游走过程，并成功复现了Kolmogorov谱、Press-Schechter质量函数和Einasto密度轮廓等关键观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为湍流与宇宙结构形成这两个看似迥异的非平衡过程提供统一的理论框架，避免依赖经验性或唯象性假设（如量纲分析），从随机动力学本质出发解释普适标度律。

Method: 构建基于尺度空间（波数k、晕质量mh、半径r）的随机游走与Fokker-Planck方程，引入尺度依赖的等待时间τ∝k^{-γ}、τ∝m_h^{-λ}、τ∝r^{-γ}，通过自洽条件确定指数γ=2/3和λ，导出能量谱、质量函数与密度轮廓。

Result: 1) 湍流中恢复Kolmogorov -5/3谱及完整能量谱（含惯性-耗散过渡、指数截断与谱常数）；2) 宇宙晕质量函数呈现双λ行为，低质量端反映初代谱，高质量端λ→2/3，匹配Press-Schechter形式；3) 单个晕密度轮廓呈双γ行为，内区由初代谱决定，外区γ→2/3对应ρ∝r^{-4/3}，整体复现Einasto剖面。

Conclusion: 湍流级联与宇宙结构形成可被纳入同一随机动力学范式——尺度空间中的广义随机游走，其核心是尺度依赖的等待时间，揭示了不同复杂系统中普适标度律的共同起源。

Abstract: We develop a unified stochastic framework for nonequilibrium systems and apply it to turbulence and cosmic structure formation. Spectra and distributions evolve by transport in "scale space", described by random walks and Fokker-Planck equations with scale-dependent waiting times. We reformulate the turbulent cascade as a random walk in wavenumber space with waiting time $τ_{gk}\propto k^{-γ}$. With $γ=2/3$ fixed by self-consistency, we recover the Kolmogorov $-5/3$ law and obtain the full energy spectrum, including the inertial-dissipation crossover, exponential cutoff and spectrum constant, without applying dimensional analysis to the spectrum itself. In bottom-up hierarchical structure formation, low-mass haloes collapse early and retain primordial imprints, while prolonged assembly near the characteristic mass $m_h^*$ erases this memory and establishes a universal spectrum $P(k)\propto k^{-1}$. We model this as a random walk in halo mass space with $τ_g\propto m_h^{-λ}$. This yields a stretched Gaussian halo mass function that reproduces the Press-Schechter form, with small-scale scaling $f_M\propto m_h^{-λ}$ and an exponential cutoff at $m_h>m_h^*$, both set by $λ$. It also produces a double-$λ$ mass function: $λ$ reflects the primordial spectrum at low masses and approaches $2/3$ near $m_h^*$, where gravity dominates. For individual haloes, a spatial random walk with $τ_{gr}\propto r^{-γ}$ generates a double-$γ$ density profile that reproduces the Einasto profile, with small-scale scaling $ρ_r\propto r^{-2γ}$ and an exponential cutoff at $r>r_s$, both set by $γ$. Here $γ$ is fixed by the initial spectrum in the inner core and approaches $2/3$ ($ρ_r\propto r^{-4/3}$) in the gravity-dominated outskirts. This framework thus describes turbulent cascades and cosmic structure formation on a common footing.

</details>


### [97] [Tentative Excess Dispersion Measures toward DESI Galaxy Groups from Localized Fast Radio Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31805)
*Jiale Wang,Taotao Fang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过匹配117个已定位的快速射电暴（FRBs）与前景星系群目录，发现穿过星系群的视线方向比未穿过者在宇宙色散量（DM）上存在约143 pc cm⁻³的显著超额，表明星系群中存在可观测的电离重子物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用快速射电暴（FRBs）的色散量（DM）作为探测宇宙电离重子的灵敏探针，检验其视线是否穿过前景星系群时会产生可测量的DM超额。

Method: 将117个具有精确定位和宿主红移的FRBs与DESI Legacy Imaging Surveys构建的前景星系群星表交叉匹配；筛选出z_host < 0.5、位于星表覆盖天区内的23条穿过星系群和18条洁净（未穿过）视线，并进行联合拟合与模板拟合分析。

Result: 组交视线相对于控制样本在宇宙DM上呈现143.3^{+53.2}_{-53.0} pc cm⁻³的正向偏移；星系晕模板拟合得到幅度A_halo = 0.34^{+0.51}_{-0.50}，后验中值为正但约束较弱；模拟预测更大样本可提升晕贡献约束。

Conclusion: 观测支持前景星系群对FRB色散量有可观测贡献，证实其含有电离重子物质；当前样本已给出显著信号，未来更多定位FRBs将更精确刻画星系晕的电子分布。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) provide sensitive probes of ionized baryons through their dispersion measures (DMs). In this work, we test whether localized FRB sight lines intersecting foreground galaxy groups exhibit a measurable excess in cosmic DM relative to clean nonintersecting sight lines. We crossmatch 117 localized FRBs with measured DMs and host redshifts against a foreground group catalog from the DESI Legacy Imaging Surveys. Within the catalog footprint and at $z_{\rm host}<0.5$, we identify 23 sight lines intersecting foreground groups and 18 clean nonintersecting sight lines for the main comparison. A joint fit to this sample gives a positive offset of $143.3^{+53.2}_{-53.0}~\mathrm{pc\,cm^{-3}}$ in the inferred cosmic DM, $\mathrm{DM}_{\rm cosmic}$, for the group-intersecting sight lines relative to the nonintersecting control sample. A template fit for the foreground halos gives $A_{\rm halo}=0.34^{+0.51}_{-0.50}$, with a positive posterior median but a weakly constrained detailed halo contribution. A mock forecast within the DESI footprint indicates that larger samples of localized FRBs would improve the foreground halo template constraint.

</details>


### [98] [Robust Measurements of the Cosmic Microwave Background Lensing Power Spectrum in the Presence of Anisotropic Cosmic Birefringence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32120)
*Rui Zhou,Michael Doohan,Christian L. Reichardt*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了各向异性宇宙双折射对CMB透镜效应测量的影响，并在MUSE框架下提出联合估计方法，以消除偏差并提高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高灵敏度CMB偏振巡天的科学潜力要求透镜分析对未建模的偏振场畸变（如各向异性宇宙双折射）具有鲁棒性，而后者可能源于轴子类场等宇称破缺物理。

Method: 在边际无偏分数展开（MUSE）框架下，量化了已建模和未建模各向异性双折射对CMB透镜测量的影响，并进行联合估计透镜与双折射的模拟分析。

Result: 忽略双折射会导致小尺度上透镜功率谱的正向偏差；联合MUSE估计可无偏恢复透镜与双折射幅度，且相比二次估计器将双折射振幅散射降低1.6–2.4倍。

Conclusion: MUSE联合场重建可在消除偏差的同时提升精度，是未来CMB数据分析中兼顾鲁棒性与精度的有效方法。

Abstract: Achieving the scientific potential of upcoming high-sensitivity cosmic microwave background (CMB) polarization surveys requires lensing analyses that are robust to unmodeled distortions of the polarization field. One such possible distortion is anisotropic cosmic birefringence, a direction-dependent rotation of the linear polarization plane that may arise from parity-violating physics such as axion-like fields. We quantify the impact of both modeled and unmodeled anisotropic birefringence on the CMB lensing measurement in the Marginal Unbiased Score Expansion (MUSE) framework. Considering noise levels expected for current and future CMB surveys, we find that neglecting birefringence produces a positive bias in the lensing bandpowers that increases towards small angular scales. For a scale-invariant birefringence power spectrum $L(L+1)C_L^{αα}/2π=10^{-5}\,{\rm rad}^2$, the positive fractional bias can reach $(10.1\pm1.1)\%$ of the lensing power at $1375<L<2000$. Jointly estimating lensing and birefringence in the MUSE framework recovers the input lensing bandpowers and birefringence amplitude without detectable bias across all tested configurations. The joint MUSE estimator also reduces the scatter of the recovered birefringence amplitude by factors of $1.6\pm0.2$ to $2.4\pm0.3$ relative to a joint quadratic estimator, with the largest gains in the highest signal-to-noise configurations. These results indicate that a joint-field reconstruction with MUSE can recover unbiased estimates of the lensing and birefringence power spectra while still improving precision relative to quadratic estimators.

</details>


### [99] [Assessing the total mass of the Planck protocluster candidates with CMB lensing stacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32282)
*Tanguy Dusserre,Hervé Dole,Mathieu Langer,Giulio Fabbian*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用阿塔卡马宇宙学望远镜（ACT）对普朗克卫星发现的高红移原星系团候选体进行CMB透镜信号叠加分析，首次直接测量其总质量，结果支持这些候选体主要是z≥1的原星系团而非已并合的成熟星系团。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原星系团是研究大尺度结构演化和宇宙恒星形成历史的关键目标，但此前仅能观测其成员星系；其大部分质量（暗物质与星系际冷气体）尚未被直接探测，亟需新方法对其总质量进行测量。

Method: 利用ACT观测的宇宙微波背景（CMB）引力透镜数据，在普朗克卫星提供的2151个原星系团候选体（其中1012个位于ACT观测天区）位置进行信号叠加，提取平均收敛（convergence）信号，并据此估算视线方向上的平均质量。

Result: 叠加中心处CMB透镜信号达12σ显著性；推得视线方向平均质量为(6.6±1.4)×10¹⁴ M⊙，与数值模拟预期一致；结合普朗克给出的红移约束1.5 < z < 3，排除了该样本主要由已弛豫的大质量结构构成的可能性。

Conclusion: 这是首次以如此直接方式测量普朗克高红移源的质量，结果强有力地支持该样本主体为z≥1的原星系团，为原星系团的物理性质与宇宙学意义提供了关键观测证据。

Abstract: Galaxy clusters in formation, or protoclusters, are important sites regarding the evolution of large scale structures and the star formation history of the Universe. With a few exceptions, only their member galaxies have been routinely observed so far. The majority of their mass is expected to be under the form of non baryonic dark matter and cold intergalactic gas. The Planck high redshift star-forming field sample compiles 2151 protocluster candidates, among which 1012 fall within the footprint of the Atacama Cosmology Telescope (ACT). The lensing of the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) by foreground structures allows us to try and assess the total mass of these protoclusters for the first time. We stack the CMB lensing data from ACT at the location of the Planck protocluster candidates to obtain a mean convergence signal. We then provide an estimate for the mean mass along the line of sight for these objects. The centre of the stacked field corresponds to a lensing signal at a $12\,σ$ significance level. The integration of the mass along the line of sight yields a value of $(6.6\pm 1.4) \times 10^{14}\,M_\odot$, similar to what is expected from numerical simulations. With the constraint $1.5 < z < 3$ from Planck, we are able to rule out the possibility that this sample is populated by virialised massive structures. This is the first time that the mass of Planck high redshift sources is measured in such a direct way. The results we obtain constitute a new strong argument in favour of this sample being composed of a majority of galaxy protoclusters lying at $z \geq 1$.

</details>


### [100] [Resolving the Hubble Tension at $0.1σ$ with Early Dark Energy: Key Role of the Highest-redshift Galaxies from JWST Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32552)
*Guo-Hong Du,Tian-Nuo Li,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过结合JWST高红移星系观测、CMB和DESI BAO数据，在轴子早期暗能量（EDE）框架下将哈勃常数H₀测得为72.76±1.00 km/s/Mpc，与SH0ES结果在0.1σ水平一致，从而缓解哈勃张力；关键机制是z=11和12.5处增强的星系丰度推动了对更强小尺度物质功率的需求，进而通过ns、f_EDE与H₀的正相关及谱指数跑动α_s显著提升H₀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前宇宙学中哈勃常数测量值（如SH0ES与CMB推断值）之间的显著张力（Hubble tension）。

Method: 在轴子早期暗能量（EDE）模型框架下，联合分析JWST在z≈11–12.5测得的紫外光度函数（考虑其参考宇宙学）、Planck CMB数据以及DESI重子声学振荡（BAO）数据；同时探索原初标量谱指数ns及其跑动αs对H₀的影响。

Result: 在EDE模型中，联合数据给出H₀ = 72.76 ± 1.00 km/s/Mpc（与SH0ES在0.1σ一致）；引入谱指数跑动αs后，H₀进一步升至73.75 ± 1.04 km/s/Mpc（超过SH0ES值），且αs > 0在4σ以上显著。

Conclusion: JWST高红移星系丰度对小尺度功率的约束，通过与EDE参数（尤其是f_EDE和ns）及原初谱参数的强简并，可显著提升H₀推断值；这揭示了高红移结构观测在缓解哈勃张力中的关键作用，并暗示原初功率谱可能存在非平凡的尺度依赖性。

Abstract: We show that the Hubble tension can be resolved at the $0.1σ$ level within the early dark energy (EDE) framework, with the highest-redshift galaxies from the JWST observations playing a key role. By accounting for the reference cosmologies of the JWST ultraviolet luminosity function measurements and combining them with cosmic microwave background (CMB) and DESI baryon acoustic oscillation data, we obtain $H_0=72.76\pm1.00~\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ in the axion EDE model, in excellent agreement with SH0ES at the $0.1σ$ level. We find that the enhanced galaxy abundances at $z=11$ and $12.5$ play a key role in driving this result. These observations favor stronger small-scale matter power, which can be accommodated by increasing the primordial scalar spectral index $n_{\rm s}$. Under the CMB constraints, the positive correlations among $n_{\rm s}$, $f_{\rm EDE}$, and $H_0$ lead to a higher inferred $H_0$. Allowing additional scale dependence in the primordial power spectrum through $α_{\rm s}$ yields a positive running at more than $4σ$, further increasing $H_0$ to $73.75\pm1.04~\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, with its value even exceeding the SH0ES measurement. These results reveal how high-redshift small-scale structure observations influence the inferred $H_0$ through the interplay between the primordial power spectrum and EDE parameter degeneracies.

</details>


### [101] [Multiscale Zoom-in Lattice Simulation for Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32753)
*Xinpeng Wang,Angelo Caravano,Leander Thiele,Misao Sasaki*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三维‘缩放进入’（zoom-in）格点方法，用于在暴胀过程中跨越更宽的尺度范围（从亚视界到穿越视界）模拟非线性涨落演化，显著延长了格点模拟可覆盖的e-folds数（从几倍增至数十倍），并在Starobinsky模型和含振荡特征模型中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统格点模拟受限于固定网格，难以同时覆盖暴胀中广泛相关的物理尺度，导致只能模拟少数e-folds；亟需一种能动态扩展分辨率与尺度覆盖范围的方法。

Method: 提出三维zoom-in格点方法：每若干e-folds对网格进行局部细化，生成子网格；小尺度功率被注入，大尺度涨落继承自上一代网格；每次zoom保持动态范围不变但平移解析波段。

Result: 在50 e-folds的Starobinsky基准测试中，曲率功率谱与线性理论一致；在含振荡特征模型中，成功复现高分辨固定网格中的峰值，并揭示出尺度依赖的非高斯性。

Conclusion: zoom-in方法显著提升了格点模拟在暴胀非线性演化研究中的尺度跨度与时长能力，为精确刻画早期宇宙非高斯信号提供了可靠数值工具。

Abstract: Lattice simulations are a powerful tool for studying the nonlinear evolution of inflationary fluctuations. The exponential expansion, however, makes it challenging to cover a broad range of scales relevant for the dynamics, limiting how many e-folds a single fixed grid can follow. We develop a three-dimensional zoom-in lattice method to follow inflationary fluctuations over a broad range of scales from the adiabatic subhorizon regime through horizon crossing. Every few e-folds, the grid is refined into one or more subgrids, with small-scale power injected and large-scale fluctuations inherited from previous generations. Each zoom shifts the resolved band while keeping its dynamic range fixed. This extends the duration over which lattice simulations can follow inflationary fluctuations from a few e-folds to tens of e-folds. In a Starobinsky benchmark evolved for 50 e-folds, the curvature power spectrum agrees with linear theory. In a model with an oscillatory feature, the zoom-in simulations recover the peak seen in a high-resolution fixed-grid run and show scale-dependent non-Gaussianity.

</details>


### [102] [Modified recombination or dynamical dark energy (or both)?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32857)
*Alexander Reeves,Gabriel P. Lynch,Andrina Nicola,Jens Chluba,Alexandre Refregier*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在联合分析CMB和BAO数据时，对再复合过程修改的暗示是否在考虑宇宙晚期膨胀历史偏离时依然存在。研究发现，当引入动力学暗能量（DDE）模型后，对早期再复合的偏好消失，且DDE模型在所有数据组合中具有最低的AIC值；添加再复合自由度反而使AIC升高并被贝叶斯证据所排斥。结果表明，此前观察到的再复合修改偏好实为CMB与BAO数据间的张力所致，而非标准再复合物理的独立偏离证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期联合CMB与BAO数据分析提示可能存在再复合过程的修改，但该信号是否真实反映物理偏离，抑或源于ΛCDM框架下CMB与BAO之间的张力，尚不明确。本文旨在检验该信号在放宽晚期宇宙膨胀历史假设（如引入动力学暗能量）时是否稳健。

Method: 分别及联合拟合七参数自由电子分数重构模型与$w_0w_a$暗能量模型，使用DESI DR2 BAO、5×2pt星系聚类+CMB透镜化数据，以及P-ACT、Planck PR4和Planck 2018三组CMB似然；通过AIC与贝叶斯证据比较模型优劣，并借助无噪声模拟数据验证因果解释。

Result: 在ΛCDM下，基线数据组合偏好早期再复合；引入DDE后该偏好消失，且$w_0$、$w_a$约束几乎不受再复合自由度影响；DDE模型AIC最低，加入再复合自由度使其AIC上升5.1–11.7并被贝叶斯证据否定；模拟表明：用DDE生成的数据误拟合为修改再复合，反之则不成立。

Conclusion: 观测到的再复合修改偏好本质上是CMB与BAO数据在ΛCDM下的不一致性所致，并非对标准再复合物理的独立挑战；动力学暗能量是更简洁、更受支持的解释。

Abstract: Several recent analyses report a hint of modified recombination when the latest cosmic microwave background (CMB) and baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) data are jointly analysed. We examine whether this preference persists when allowing for departures in the late-time expansion history of the Universe. We fit a seven-parameter reconstruction of the free-electron fraction and a $w_0w_a$ dark-energy model, separately and jointly, to DESI DR2 BAO, a $5\times2$pt combination of galaxy clustering and CMB lensing, and three CMB likelihoods: P-ACT, Planck PR4 and Planck 2018. With a cosmological constant, our baseline data combination favours early recombination near last scattering, agreeing with previous analyses. Allowing for dynamical dark energy (DDE) removes this preference, while the constraints on $w_0$ and $w_a$ change little when the recombination history is marginalised over. DDE has the lowest Akaike information criterion of the models we consider for every data combination, and adding recombination freedom to it raises the AIC by $5.1$--$11.7$ and is disfavoured by the Bayesian evidence. We interpret these results in terms of CMB predictions for the BAO distances: earlier recombination and DDE both shift these predictions towards the measurements, but only DDE also changes the redshift dependence of the late-time expansion, which anisotropic BAO measurements constrain. We support these conclusions using noiseless mock data, finding that fitting modified recombination to a mock Universe generated with DDE reproduces the apparent preference for modified recombination, whereas DDE cannot similarly reproduce the signatures of modified recombination. The preference for modified recombination found with a cosmological constant therefore reflects the discrepancy between the CMB and BAO, rather than independent evidence for departures from standard recombination physics.

</details>


### [103] [Lensing ratios as a geometric probe of dark energy in the LSST and Simons Observatory era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32861)
*Judit Prat,Carles Sánchez*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出并优化了星系-宇宙微波背景（CMB）与星系-星系透镜效应比值（LRs）作为纯几何探针，用于约束暗能量与空间曲率，在LSST Y10和Simons Observatory精度下展现出对BAO的强互补性及在扩展宇宙学模型中的独特约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用无需标准烛光或标准尺的纯几何方法探测宇宙距离，突破传统测距方法对物理解释的依赖，尤其在高红移和曲率-暗能量简并问题中提供新途径。

Method: 建立LRs的几何极限理论框架，控制内禀对齐与透镜放大等系统误差；结合LSST Y10与Simons Observatory观测精度进行端到端预报；通过FOM分析、leave-one-out检验及七参数暗能量密度重建评估其对宇宙学参数的约束能力。

Result: LSST Y10中LRs单个比值测量精度达1.0%，总信噪比360；在w0-wa参数空间中，相较CMB+SN+BAO组合提升FOM 15%；可替代BAO，使CMB+SN+FOM达完整组合的66%；在七-bin暗能量重建中z>1处减小误差达16%，自由曲率且仅用CMB角度尺度时减小误差达50%。

Conclusion: LRs是一种高精度、强鲁棒性的独立几何探针，特别适用于打破曲率与暗能量简并、增强高红移约束，并在多探针联合分析中显著提升宇宙学参数限制能力。

Abstract: Ratios of galaxy-CMB and galaxy-galaxy lensing around the same foreground galaxies probe relative distances extending to recombination without a standard candle or ruler. For sufficiently narrow foreground redshift distributions, and with intrinsic alignments and lens magnification controlled, these lensing ratios (LRs) become geometrical. Here we characterize the geometrical limit and develop an end-to-end optimized framework for the probe with LSST Y10 and Simons Observatory uncertainties. In our forecast for LSST Y10, the individual ratios are measured to a median precision of 1.0%, with a total signal-to-noise ratio of 360. We then study the complementarity of LRs as an additional geometrical probe to constrain dark energy and curvature. We find that LRs improve the $w_0$-$w_a$ figure of merit of the combined CMB, Type Ia supernova, and baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) data by 15%, after marginalizing over systematic uncertainties. As an independent geometric probe, they also enable competitive leave-one-out tests, in which one of the probes is dropped, and we find they are most effective at replacing the BAO: adding LRs to CMB+supernova raises the figure of merit from 33% to 66% of that of the full CMB+supernova+BAO combination. We also find LRs are especially valuable in extended model spaces constrained with minimal assumptions: in a seven-bin reconstruction of the dark-energy density, they reduce per-bin uncertainties by up to 16% at $z>1$, and by up to 50% when freeing curvature and dropping the non-geometrical part of the CMB likelihood (i.e. keeping the acoustic angular scale part), where they are able to break the degeneracy between curvature and dark energy.

</details>


### [104] [VENUS: A Census of Strong Lensing in a 60-Cluster JWST Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33142)

Diego,Henry C. Ferguson,Steven L. Finkelstein,Tiger Yu-Yang Hsiao,Yolanda Jiménez-Teja,José M. Palencia,Gregor Rihtaršič,Chris Willott,Rogier A. Windhorst,Hollis B. Akins,Ricardo O. Amorín,Larry D. Bradley,Chloe M. Cheng,Christopher J. Conselice,David A. Coulter,Pratika Dayal,Guillaume Desprez,Miroslava Dessauges-Zavadsky,Andreas L. Faisst,Qinyue Fei,Brenda Frye,Yuichi Harikane,Jeyhan S. Kartaltepe,Tomokazu Kiyota,Conor Larison,Paulo A. A. Lopes,Ray A. Lucas,Georgios E. Magdis,Nicholas S. Martis,Masami Ouchi,Richard Pan,Casey Papovich,Massimo Pascale,Justin D. R. Pierel,Tom Resseguier,Johan Richard,Massimo Ricotti,Luke Robbins,William Sheu,Fengwu Sun,Felix Vecchi,Brian Welch,Hiroto Yanagisawa*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文基于JWST VENUS计划第四周期观测的57个星系团，构建了目前最大、最均匀的强引力透镜模型样本，实现了高精度图像位置拟合，并给出了关键透镜参数与星系团质量之间的标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建大样本、高质量、均匀的星系团强引力透镜模型，以支持高红移天体研究并建立透镜可观测量与星系团物理质量之间的定量关系。

Method: 利用VENUS项目获得的57个星系团的JWST观测数据（共5013个像、1733个多重成像系统），使用自研软件AstroLensPy构建参数化强透镜模型，并以平均像平面精度Δ_RMS = 0.54″评估拟合质量；同时分析Einstein半径、放大面积等透镜量与SZ/X射线推断的质量M_500,c之间的标度关系。

Result: 获得了迄今最大、最均匀的星系团强透镜模型集合（总成像面积576 arcmin²）；确定MACS J0717.5+3745等三个星系团为目前已知最强引力望远镜（z_s=10时|μ|≥3的放大面积≥9 arcmin²）；首次基于大样本导出Einstein半径、放大面积等与M_500,c的统计标度关系。

Conclusion: VENUS强透镜模型样本显著提升了对高红移宇宙的探测能力，并为星系团质量校准和透镜理论检验提供了关键实测基准。

Abstract: We present strong-lensing models for the 57 galaxy clusters observed in Cycle 4 out of the 60 targeted by the JWST Vast Exploration for Nascent, Unexplored Sources (VENUS) program. Models for the remaining three clusters will be incorporated after their observations. The dataset comprises 5013 individual images, corresponding to 1733 multiply-imaged systems, of which 912 have spectroscopic redshift measurements and 227 are newly identified. Using this new wealth of observations, we construct parametric lens models with our $\mathtt{AstroLensPy}$ software, reproducing the observed image positions with an average image-plane precision $Δ_{\rm RMS}$ of $0.54''$. With a total imaging area of $576$ arcmin$^2$, VENUS offers the largest homogeneous set of cluster strong-lensing models to date. The clusters yield a combined area of $191$ arcmin$^{2}$ magnified by $|μ|\geq3$ for a source $z_s=10$, for example, establishing the VENUS fields as a premier survey for high-redshift studies. Among our sample, the clusters MACS J0717.5+3745, RXC J0600.1-2007 and PLCK G287.0+32.9 display the largest Einstein radii ($\geq 60''$) and magnified areas ($\mathcal{A}_{z_s=10}^{|μ|\geq3} \geq 9$ arcmin$^2$) for a source $z_s=10$, and therefore constitute the most potent gravitational telescopes known to date. Finally, leveraging the statistical power of the sample, we derive scaling relations between various lensing observables (Einstein radius, magnified area, projected mass) and the SZ/X-ray-inferred cluster mass, $M_{500,c}$.

</details>


### [105] [Initial Conditions and Wave-Phase Resolution for Scalar-Field Dark Matter Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33083)
*Hafiz Inam Ullah,Muhammad Awais,Yourong Frank Wang,Tonatiuh Matos*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种匹配ΛCDM与标量场暗物质（SFDM）的初始化框架，用于波基宇宙学模拟；结果表明，尽管密度功率谱可被高精度再现，但波函数相位的奈奎斯特采样不足问题显著，需更高分辨率才能保证相位准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波基标量场暗物质（SFDM）模拟需要同时精确初始化密度和复波函数相位，而现有方法在相位分辨率方面缺乏充分验证。

Method: 构建了连接CLASS-SFDM、MUSIC和AxioNyx的初始化框架；通过不同网格分辨率（256³、512³、1024³）对比分析密度功率谱、速度散度、相位增量及相位相关性等指标。

Result: 在k∈[8,13.4] h/Mpc范围内，256³模拟密度功率谱再现误差仅0.138%；但相位增量违反奈奎斯特准则的比例从256³的46.7%降至1024³的0.124%；512³与1024³间密度场强相关（rδ=0.9989），但相位差中位数达0.264 rad。

Conclusion: 线性密度场的高保真再现不能保证波函数相位足够解析；必须采用更高空间分辨率或更严格的相位初始化标准，以确保SFDM非线性演化模拟的可靠性。

Abstract: Wave-based cosmological simulations of scalar-field dark matter (SFDM) require accurate initialization of both density and complex-wavefunction phase. We develop and validate a matched $Λ$CDM--SFDM initialization framework connecting CLASS-SFDM, MUSIC, and AxioNyx. For quadratic SFDM with $m_φ=3.1623\times10^{-22}\,\mathrm{eV}$ at $z_{\rm ini}=120$, the scalar-component half-power scale is $k_{50}^φ\simeq10.54\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$. An independent AxiECAMB comparison gives a normalized RMS deviation of $0.328\%$ in the synchronous-gauge SFDM velocity divergence over $0.20944\leq k\leq13.4\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$. A $256^3$ MUSIC realization reproduces the CLASS density power spectrum over $8\leq k\leq13.404\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ with a median ratio of $1.00131$ and an RMS fractional deviation of $0.138\%$. Despite this agreement, full-volume phase diagnostics show that the fraction of velocity-implied phase increments violating the Nyquist criterion decreases from $46.7\%$ at $256^3$ to $13.685\%$ at $512^3$ and $0.124060\%$ at $1024^3$. A separately generated realization yields $13.7299\%$ and $0.124983\%$ at $512^3$ and $1024^3$, respectively, with relative differences below $1\%$. In a common-parent $512^3$--$1024^3$ comparison, the central-plane density fields remain strongly correlated ($r_δ=0.9989$). The median absolute circular phase difference is $0.264$ rad without masking and $0.246$ rad for $86.6\%$ of blocks satisfying $R\geq0.5$, with 90th percentiles $0.826$ and $0.753$ rad. These results demonstrate that accurate linear density recovery does not guarantee adequate wave-phase resolution and provide validation criteria for SFDM initial conditions prior to nonlinear evolution.

</details>

### [106] [Light-cone distributions of vector and scalar bound states in the Schwinger model from tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31745)
*Juan Hormaza,Wenyang Qian*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用张量网络方法，在Minkowski时空直接计算了含质量Schwinger模型中矢量与标量介子的轻锥分布函数（PDFs）和轻锥分布振幅（LCDAs），揭示了其由电荷共轭决定的不同轻锥结构特征，并验证了张量网络在低维规范理论中研究强子轻锥结构的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻锥分布编码了强子的组分结构，但涉及欧氏格点方法无法直接获取的实时关联；需要发展能在Minkowski时空直接计算轻锥分布的新方法。

Method: 采用消去规范场的Kogut-Susskind格点哈密顿量，以矩阵乘积态（MPS）制备真空及介子态，通过电荷共轭和动量识别矢量/标量介子，并在Minkowski空间中通过实时演化直接计算规范不变的轻锥关联函数。

Result: 发现矢量介子PDF单峰、LCDA对称；标量介子PDF双峰、LCDA反对称（零矩为零，需一阶矩归一化）；两种介子的衰变常数从局域矩阵元和LCDA矩得到一致结果；大质量下PDF近似等于LCDA平方，表明价态主导。

Conclusion: 张量网络是研究低维规范理论中基态及激发强子轻锥分布的一种实用可行方法。

Abstract: Light-cone distributions encode the partonic structure of hadrons, but they involve real-time correlations that Euclidean lattice methods cannot access directly. We compute the parton distribution functions (PDFs) and light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) of the vector and scalar meson bound states of the massive Schwinger model with tensor networks. Using the Kogut-Susskind lattice Hamiltonian with the gauge field eliminated through Gauss's law, we prepare the vacuum and the mesons as matrix product states and identify the vector and scalar mesons through their charge conjugation and momentum. The gauge-invariant light-cone correlators that define both distributions are evaluated directly in Minkowski space by real-time evolution. The two mesons exhibit qualitatively different light-cone structure, dictated by charge conjugation. The vector meson has a single-peaked PDF and a symmetric LCDA, while the scalar meson has a double-peaked PDF and an antisymmetric LCDA, whose vanishing zeroth moment requires a normalization through its first moment. The decay constants obtained from local matrix elements and from the moments of the LCDAs agree, and approach predictions from nonrelativistic model as the fermion mass increases. For the relatively large masses considered in this work, the PDFs closely follow the squared LCDAs, showing that the mesons are dominated by their valence component. Our results demonstrate tensor networks as a practical route to light-cone distributions of ground-state as well as excited hadrons in low-dimensional gauge theories.

</details>


### [107] [Parton Physics in the Schwinger Model from Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31749)
*Mari Carmen Bañuls,Krzysztof Cichy,C. -J. David Lin,Manuel Schneider*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在闵可夫斯基时空哈密顿框架下直接计算部分子分布函数（PDFs）和轻锥分布振幅（LCDAs）的新方法，利用张量网络（MPS）表示希尔伯特空间态，并在施温格模型中验证了该方法对不同费米子质量下矢量介子相关量的计算有效性与系统可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，PDFs 和 LCDAs 因其定义中包含沿光前方向的威尔逊线，难以在欧几里得格点计算中直接获取；本文旨在发展一种可在闵可夫斯基时空直接计算这些非微扰结构的方法。

Method: 采用哈密顿量框架结合张量网络（矩阵乘积态，MPS）表示量子态，通过交替进行外源电荷的空间平移与 Trotter 化时间演化来实现光前威尔逊线；系统性地控制并外推格点间距、体积、键维数、规范场截断及 Trotter 步长等误差源。

Result: 成功计算了施温格模型中费米子/反费米子的 PDFs、LCDAs 及矢量介子衰变常数；验证了归一化、动量求和规则和对称性；发现纠缠熵增长缓慢，中等键维数已足够。

Conclusion: 该方法为在更一般强子体系中利用张量网络计算光前矩阵元提供了可行路径，具有良好的系统误差控制能力和扩展前景。

Abstract: Parton distribution functions (PDFs) and light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) encode the non-perturbative structure of hadrons in terms of their partonic constituents. They are defined through matrix elements containing parton operators that are connected by a Wilson line along a light-front direction, which makes them difficult to access in Euclidean lattice calculations. We demonstrate how parton functions can be directly obtained in Minkowski space using the Hamiltonian framework. We compute fermion and antifermion PDFs, LCDAs, and decay constants of the vector meson in the Schwinger model for several fermion masses. States in Hilbert space are efficiently represented by tensor networks, with an expressivity that is systematically controlled by a bond dimension. The light-front Wilson line is implemented as a stepwise evolution on the lattice that alternates spatial shifts of an external charge with Trotterized time-evolution steps acting on a matrix product state. All systematic uncertainties are quantified by varying and extrapolating in the lattice spacing, volume, bond dimension, gauge-field truncation, and Trotter step width. The slow growth of the entanglement entropy makes moderate bond dimensions sufficient, which supports the feasibility of using tensor networks for computing light-front matrix elements in more general settings. We confirm that the computed distributions fulfill the expected normalizations, momentum sum rules, and symmetry properties.

</details>


### [108] [Intrinsic Soft Function in the Continuum and Physical Mass Limit from Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31810)
*Zhi-Chao Gong,Min-Huan Chu,Jun Hua,Xiangdong Ji,Andreas Schäfer,Jin-Xin Tan,Han-Zhang Wang,Wei Wang,Jian-Hui Zhang,Mu-Hua Zhang,Qi-An Zhang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次在格点QCD中同时控制连续极限、物理π介子质量极限和无穷动量极限，利用大动量有效理论，从轻介子形状因子和准TMD波函数中提取本征软函数，并发现其横距依赖呈近似指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征软函数是横动量依赖（TMD）因子化中普适的非微扰成分，但此前尚无同时满足连续极限、物理π介子质量极限和无穷动量极限的格点QCD确定结果。

Method: 采用大动量有效理论（LaMET），结合轻介子形状因子与准TMD波函数；引入幂次计数引导的轴矢量插值算符，在物理π质量下直接计算；使用覆盖四种格距的六个组态进行外推。

Result: 实现了至横距离b_⊥ ≈ 1.1 fm的精确确定，短距行为与微扰预言一致；横距依赖呈近似指数衰减，对应有效尺度Λ ≈ 0.7 GeV（μ = 2 GeV）。

Conclusion: 该结果为威尔逊线非微扰动力学提供了定量基准，是高精度TMD研究的关键输入。

Abstract: The intrinsic soft function is a universal nonperturbative ingredient of transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) factorization. Despite substantial progress in lattice QCD studies, a determination with simultaneous control of the continuum, physical-pion-mass, and infinite-momentum limits has remained unavailable. In this work we present the first lattice determination incorporating all these limits within large-momentum effective theory, extracting the intrinsic soft function from a light-meson form factor and quasi-TMD wave functions. A key advance is the use of power-counting-guided axial-vector interpolators, which substantially improve the signal and enable a direct calculation at the physical pion mass. Combined with results from six ensembles spanning four lattice spacings, this improvement allows a precise determination extending to $b_\perp\simeq1.1~\mathrm{fm}$, while remaining consistent with perturbative predictions at short distances. The resulting transverse-distance dependence exhibits an approximately exponential decay, with the decay rate being characterized by an effective scale $Λ\simeq0.7~\mathrm{GeV}$ at $μ=2~\mathrm{GeV}$, providing a quantitative benchmark for nonperturbative Wilson-line dynamics and essential input for precision TMD studies.

</details>


### [109] [Bound-state preparation in strongly coupled theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33079)
*Scott Lawrence,Duff Neill*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需量子相位估计、利用弱耦合辅助场来制备强耦合理论中渐近态（如束缚态）的新算法族，并在1维非相对论理论中通过经典模拟验证，同时估算了其在QCD中J/ψ产生过程中的应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多量子模拟任务（如量子场论散射）需要制备渐近态（如束缚态），而强耦合理论中无法用绝热法从自由理论出发制备，亟需新方法。

Method: 提出一类基于弱耦合辅助场的复合态制备算法，适用于强耦合（包括非阿贝尔规范）理论，不依赖量子相位估计。

Result: 在1维非相对论理论中通过经典模拟成功验证该方法；并估算其在QCD中J/ψ产生过程的产率。

Conclusion: 该方法为强耦合理论中渐近态的量子制备提供了可行、高效且硬件友好的新途径。

Abstract: Many quantum simulation tasks, including scattering in quantum field theories and effective non-relativistic theories, require the preparation of asymptotic states. Such a state, which may be a bound state, is typically the lowest-lying excited state in some sector of Hilbert space. At weak coupling these states can often be prepared adiabatically by beginning with the free theory, but this is not generally feasible in strongly coupled theories. We detail a family of algorithms for preparing composite states in strongly coupled theories, including non-Abelian gauge theories, by using weakly coupled auxiliary fields. These methods do not require quantum phase estimation. We demonstrate the method numerically, via classical simulation, on nonrelativistic theories in one spatial dimension. We estimate the yield of this method applied to $J/ψ$ production in quantum chromodynamics.

</details>


### [110] [Prospects and limitations of adaptive variational quantum algorithms for lattice field theories: a case study in 2+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33138)
*Nico Dichter,Andrea Bulgarelli,Lena Funcke,Stefan Kühn*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了ADAPT-VQE算法在高于1+1维格点规范理论中的适用性，发现其存在两个普遍限制：实哈密顿量与实初态下实池算符梯度为零；易收敛至激发态。通过2+1维自由Wilson费米子模型验证，并提出引入电荷共轭对称性及组合不同算符池等改进策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验ADAPT-VQE算法能否推广至高于1+1维的格点场论，以拓展其在量子模拟中的应用范围。

Method: 理论分析ADAPT-VQE的梯度行为与收敛特性，结合2+1维自由Wilson费米子模型进行数值验证，并尝试引入电荷共轭对称性、组合不同算符池与适配初态等改进方法。

Result: 确认ADAPT-VQE在实哈密顿量和实初态下存在梯度消失与陷入激发态两大限制；在2+1D模型中观察到这两类问题；引入电荷共轭对称性和混合池策略可有效恢复基态与第一激发态。

Conclusion: ADAPT-VQE在高维格点场论中需谨慎使用，须针对性改进算符池与初态设计；本文提出的策略为后续高维应用提供了实用指南。

Abstract: The variational quantum algorithm ADAPT-VQE has previously been used to prepare the vacuum of lattice gauge theories in $1+1D$. In this paper, we study whether it extends to higher dimensions. We identify two general limitations of ADAPT-VQE. First, if the qubit Hamiltonian and the initial state are real, i.e., invariant under qubit-level time-reversal, all real-valued pool operators have vanishing gradients and are never selected. Second, the algorithm can converge to excited eigenstates. We illustrate both limitations, also providing examples from literature, and present a first application of ADAPT-VQE to a lattice field theory in $2+1D$, namely free Wilson fermions, where both obstacles occur. Adding charge conjugation symmetry to the operator pool improves the results, and combining different pools with suitable initial states recovers the ground and first excited states over the full mass range. These findings give practical guidelines for applying ADAPT-VQE to lattice field theories beyond $1+1D$.

</details>
