### [111] [Spectroscopic identification of z>7 quasars with Roman Space Telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31352)
*Mainak Singha,Wei Leong Tee,Sangeeta Malhotra,Isak Wold,James Rhoads,Xiaohui Fan*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟Roman望远镜宽场仪器光谱仪在z=7.5–10红移范围内的观测，评估其利用莱曼阿尔法断裂识别高红移类星体（QSO）的能力，并测试分类方法的可靠性与信噪限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为利用Roman望远镜光谱仪直接探测z > 7的高红移类星体，需评估其基于Lyman-alpha断裂的识别能力及观测极限。

Method: 采用ESpRESSO模拟HLWAS式曝光下的z=7.5–10类星体光谱，包含PSF卷积、无狭缝色散、源与背景泊松噪声、读出噪声及Horne最优提取；使用三种Lyman-alpha等效宽度（EW）假设，并用光谱角制图器（SAM）与M/L/T矮星和被动红星系对比分类。

Result: Lyman-alpha断裂探测完备率在J_AB ≈ 23.7–24.1时高于90%，在J_AB ≈ 24.3–24.7时达50%；固定红移SAM测试中QSO召回率TPR ≥ 0.982，且无矮星或红星系被误判为QSO；但该低假阳性率仅反映训练库内噪声鲁棒性，非独立巡天标定结果；最暗端灵敏度主要受限于Lyman-alpha高EW尾部分布。

Conclusion: Roman光谱仪具备高效探测z > 7类星体的潜力，尤其在J_AB < 24.7范围内可靠；但极暗端（J_AB > 25）结果高度依赖Lyman-alpha EW模型，需更精确的物理建模与实测校准。

Abstract: The Roman Wide Field Instrument grism will cover a large area over 1.0-1.93 micrometers, making it possible to search directly for Lyman-alpha breaks in quasars beyond redshift z = 7. We simulate quasars at z = 7.5-10 under HLWAS-like exposures with ESpRESSO, including PSF convolution, slitless dispersion, source and background Poisson noise, read noise, and Horne optimal extraction. Three Lyman-alpha equivalent-width (EW) prescriptions are used to bracket the uncertainty in line strength, and the extracted spectra are compared with M/L/T dwarfs and passive red galaxies using the spectral angle mapper. The Lyman-alpha-break completeness remains above 90% to J_AB approximately 23.7-24.1 and reaches 50% at J_AB approximately 24.3-24.7. Beyond J_AB approximately 25, the result depends strongly on the adopted EW distribution. In the fixed-redshift SAM test, the QSO recovery rate is TPR >= 0.982. None of the noisy brown-dwarf or red-galaxy test spectra is classified as a QSO. Because those test spectra are generated from the same finite parent libraries as the reference classes, this result measures the stability of the classification against noise within those libraries rather than an independently calibrated survey false-positive rate. At the faint end, the strongest astrophysical sensitivity in the present calculation is to the high-EW tail of the Lyman-alpha distribution.

</details>


### [112] [JWST/NIRSpec observations of AMORE6 suggest active Pop~III star formation in a self-polluted galaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31355)
*Mirko Curti,Elka Rusta,Stefania Salvadori,Stefano Carniani,Francesco D'Eugenio,Bianca Moreschini,Roberto Maiolino,Alessandro Marconi,Giacomo Venturi,Francesco Belfiore,Elisa Cataldi,Viola Gelli,Yuki Isobe,Xihan Ji,Mingyu Li,Robert G. Pascalau,James A. A. Trussler,Hannah Übler*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过JWST/NIRSpec对高红移星系AMORE6-B进行深度光谱观测，发现其具有极低的氧丰度（约0.04–0.4%太阳丰度）和显著的HeII发射线，支持其为处于自污染阶段的第三星族（Pop III）星系；光致电离模型更倾向金属自由、初质量函数（IMF）偏重的恒星种群，或含硬X射线源的贫金属种群，排除了常规贫金属模型等其他解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证AMORE6是否为真正金属自由（Pop III）星系，因其先前被提出为无[OIII]发射的金属自由候选体，但需更高信噪比和分辨率的光谱确认其真实金属丰度与电离机制。

Method: 利用JWST/NIRSpec对强引力透镜放大的AMORE6-B图像开展深度积分光谱观测，测量HeII、Hβ、[OIII]、CIV等谱线强度与等效宽度，结合电子温度、氦丰度、消光及光致电离模型，约束恒星种群性质（如IMF形状、金属丰度、年龄）与电离源类型。

Result: 检测到强HeIIλ4686（EW=35±8 Å，HeII/Hβ=0.080±0.015），弱[OIII]（R3=0.25）和CIV，无其他金属线；推得12+log(O/H)≈6.08^{+0.24}_{-0.80}（当前最低质量星系中最低氧丰度），C/O接近或高于太阳值；HeII/Balmer比限制Pop III IMF特征质量M_ch ≲ 70 M⊙（Salpeter斜率下）；最佳拟合为自污染Pop III模型：~1.3×10⁵ M⊙ Pop III恒星形成于近4 Myr内，电离轻微富集气体并泄漏86%电离光子。

Conclusion: AMORE6-B是迄今最有力的高红移Pop III星系候选体之一，其光谱特征与自污染阶段的Pop III星系模型高度一致；虽X射线双星等替代模型不能完全排除，但需极端假设；结果为早期宇宙第一代恒星形成与化学增丰提供了关键观测约束。

Abstract: AMORE6 is a strongly lensed ($μ\simeq78$), intrinsically faint galaxy at $z=5.725$, previously proposed as a metal-free candidate from the absence of [OIII]$λ$5007 in NIRCam slitless spectroscopy. We present deep JWST/NIRSpec spectroscopy of its brighter image, AMORE6-B. We detect HeII$λλ$1640,4686, with HeII$λ$4686/H$β=0.080\pm0.015$ and EW(HeII$λ$4686)$=35\pm8$ Å, weak [OIII] ($R_3=0.25$) and CIV$λλ$1548,51, and no other metal lines. H and He lines give negligible attenuation, $T_e\simeq2\times10^4$ K, and $Y=0.226^{+0.022}_{-0.024}$. The oxygen abundance, $12+\log(O/H)\simeq6.08^{+0.24}_{-0.80}$ (0.04-0.4% $Z_\odot$, depending on the calibration), is the lowest at its stellar mass, while C/O is near or above solar. Photoionization models of different ionizing sources favour metal-free stars with a top-heavy initial mass function (IMF), as in the self-polluted phase of simulated Population III (Pop III) galaxies, or a metal-poor population with a luminous hard X-ray source; ordinary metal-poor populations, very massive stars, and accretion-based sources are disfavoured. A full SED fit in the fiducial self-polluted scenario gives $1.3\times10^5\,M_\odot$ of Pop III stars formed within the last 4 Myr, which ionize mildly enriched gas and leak 86% of their ionizing photons. The HeII-to-Balmer ratio constrains the slope $x$ and characteristic mass $M_{ch}$ of the Pop III IMF jointly, to $90x-268\lesssim M_{ch}/M_\odot\lesssim172x-330$ ($3σ$; $M_{ch}\lesssim70\,M_\odot$ for a Salpeter slope), excluding the most top-heavy IMFs allowed by near-field constraints. X-ray binaries are a possible alternative, but require either a metal-poor population too young (1 Myr) to host compact objects and an X-ray luminosity far above that of metal-poor galaxies, or $2\times10^6\,M_\odot$ of older Pop III.2 stars whose metals were mostly expelled.

</details>


### [113] [AGN feedback in the multiphase inner intracluster medium of a cosmological super-resolved galaxy cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31367)
*Marine Prunier,Rahul Ramesh,Dylan Nelson,Annalisa Pillepich,Julie Hlavacek-Larrondo,Katrin Lehle,Joey Braspenning*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率宇宙学磁流体动力学模拟，研究了低质量冷核星系团中冷却气体云和丝状结构的性质及其与活动星系核（AGN）反馈的相互作用，发现AGN反馈虽能碎化并再分布冷气体，但不破坏其整体储备，并可能在局部促进热不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷核星系团核心中的星系际介质（ICM）是受活动星系核（AGN）反馈调控的多相环境，其中延伸的冷气体云 nebulae 的起源及其与AGN反馈的相互作用仍不清楚。

Method: 采用TNG-Cluster初始条件和IllustrisTNG星系形成模型，开展低质量冷核星系团（M200c=2.3×10^14 M⊙）的宇宙学磁流体动力学zoom-in模拟；通过超拉格朗日加密，在中心370 ckpc内实现冷相气体（10^4 K < T < 10^5.5 K）约40–90 pc空间分辨率和2.3×10^4 M⊙重子质量分辨率；对比开启与关闭AGN动能反馈的模拟结果。

Result: 模拟揭示了中心存在大量致密、低熵的冷气体丝状结构（含流入、流出及偶见旋转支撑盘）；AGN反馈可碎化并再分布冷气体、提高其覆盖分数，但不摧毁冷气体库；单次爆发在<50 Myr内瞬时提升冷/热气体速度与速度弥散；识别出被AGN外流抬升的低熵气体；AGN反馈既全局加热ICM抑制冷却，又通过激波压缩、混合、湍流和抬升等机制在局部促发热不稳定性。

Conclusion: AGN反馈对冷气体并非单纯的破坏性作用，而是具有双重角色：一方面抑制全局冷却，另一方面通过多种物理过程为局部热不稳定性创造有利条件，从而维持冷气体的持续产生与演化。

Abstract: The intracluster medium (ICM) of galaxy cluster cores is a multiphase environment regulated by active galactic nuclei (AGN) feedback. Extended cool gas nebulae with complex morphologies and kinematics are commonly observed in cool-core clusters, but their origin and interaction with AGN feedback remain poorly understood. We present a cosmological magnetohydrodynamical zoom-in simulation of a low-mass cool-core galaxy cluster ($M_{\mathrm{200c}}=2.3\times10^{14}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$), using initial conditions from TNG-Cluster and the IllustrisTNG galaxy formation model. Through super-Lagrangian refinement, we achieve a baryonic mass resolution of $2.3\times10^{4}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ and a spatial resolution of $\sim40\text{-}90$ pc in the cool ($10^{4}\,\mathrm{K}<T<10^{5.5}\,\mathrm{K}$) gas phase within the central $370$ ckpc, while retaining the full cosmological context. We investigate the properties and evolution of cool clouds and filaments and their interaction with AGN feedback at $z\leq0.35$. The cluster core hosts a rich population of dense, low-entropy filaments, including inflowing and outflowing structures and occasional rotationally supported disks. Comparing simulations with AGN kinetic feedback turned off and on, we find that AGN activity fragments and redistributes the cool gas and increases its covering fraction without destroying the cool gas reservoir. Individual outbursts drive transient increases in the velocities and velocity dispersions of both cool and hot gas, on short, $\lesssim 50$ Myr, timescales. We also identify low-entropy gas uplifted by AGN-driven outflows. While feedback globally heats the ICM and offsets cooling, it also creates conditions that may favor local thermal instabilities -- through shock compressions, mixing, turbulence, and uplift.

</details>


### [114] [Low-excitation molecular gas in a Ly$α$-associated companion of the $z=4.7$ quasar in BR1202-0725](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31382)
*C. Yang,S. Muller,M. D. Lehnert,T. J. L. C. Bakx,P. Cox,G. C. Jones,K. Knudsen,C. Mazzucchelli,A. Omont,Zhi-Yu Zhang*

TL;DR: 本文通过ALMA观测发现高红移类星体BR1202-0725的Lyα伴星LAE-2中存在大量但低激发态的分子气体，其CO(5-4)/CO(2-1)线比显著低于主星系和亚毫米星系，表明气体处于接近CMB温度的弱激发状态；经CMB修正后，[C I]推得的分子气体质量与[C II]一致，而未修正的CO(5-4)会低估质量达4倍；该伴星具有冷尘埃、主序星形成特征及高气体占比，说明高红移类星体伴星可拥有易被中等J值CO巡天遗漏的大质量低温分子气体库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移类星体伴星的分子气体质量通常依赖[C II]、尘埃或单一中J值CO线（如CO(5-4)）估算，但这些方法可能因激发条件偏差导致系统性低估；亟需多谱线联合观测以校准气体质量，并理解伴星在星系演化中的真实角色。

Method: 利用ALMA探测LAE-2（z=4.7类星体BR1202-0725的Lyα伴星）的CO(5-4)和[C I](1-0)谱线，并结合文献中CO(2-1)流量、 archival远红外测光数据，开展非LTE辐射转移建模、CMB激发修正、尘埃热辐射拟合及气体/恒星质量比分析。

Result: LAE-2的CO(5-4)/CO(2-1)线比为0.22±0.04，显著低于主星系和SMG；非LTE模型显示CO(5-4)激发温度仅比CMB高约4 K；CMB修正后[C I]给出分子气体质量≈9×10¹⁰ M⊙，与[C II]一致，而未修正CO(5-4)低估4倍；尘埃温度≈25 K，属主序星形成，气体/恒星质量比≈4，耗尽时间≈0.7 Gyr。

Conclusion: 高红移类星体伴星可维持大质量、弱激发（近CMB温度）、富气体的主序星系性质；中J值CO单线探测易遗漏此类低温气体，需结合[C I]、[C II]及CMB修正以获得可靠分子气体质量估计。

Abstract: Quasar companions at high redshifts are usually gas-rich star-forming galaxies whose molecular gas masses ($M_{\mathrm{mol}}$) are estimated from [C II], dust emission or a single mid-$J$ CO line. We detect CO(5-4) and [C I](1-0) with ALMA in LAE-2, a Ly$α$-associated companion of the $z=4.7$ quasar in BR1202-0725, and CO(5-4) at the location near LAE-1, the Ly$α$ emitter between the quasar and the submillimeter galaxy (SMG). With the CO(2-1) flux from the literature, LAE-2 has an $L'_{\mathrm{CO(5-4)}}/L'_{\mathrm{CO(2-1)}}$ ratio of $0.22\pm0.04$, as in high-redshift main-sequence galaxies but four times lower than in the quasar host and the SMG. A non-LTE model places the CO(5-4) excitation temperature only 4 K above the cosmic microwave background (CMB), yet the same gas would show an even lower ratio against the $z=0$ CMB. The CMB-corrected $M_{\mathrm{mol}}$ from [C I], $\approx9\times10^{10} M_{\odot}$, agrees with the independent [C II] estimate, whereas CO(5-4) gives a four times lower mass if assumed thermalized at 40 K with the same CO-to-H$_2$ conversion factor. Combining our 3 mm measurement with archival far-infrared photometry, we find cold ($\approx25$ K) dust and main-sequence star formation, with a gas-to-stellar mass ratio of $\approx4$ and a depletion time of $\approx0.7$ Gyr. Its star formation can supply the far-ultraviolet field that the [C I], [C II], and CO lines require. Quasar companions can thus retain massive, weakly excited molecular gas reservoirs that mid-$J$ CO surveys can miss.

TL;DR: 本文报告了在红移z > 8处发现的第一个类星体EUCL J150452.291+625224.39（J1504+6252），其红移z = 8.129，对应宇宙年龄约6.3亿年；该类星体具有典型宽线区光谱特征，中心黑洞质量约为10^8.3–10^8.9倍太阳质量，处于次爱丁顿吸积状态，对早期超大质量黑洞的形成机制（如种子质量与增长效率）提出了新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索宇宙再电离时期（z > 6）超大质量黑洞（SMBH）如何在极短时间内形成，检验现有黑洞种子与增长模型。

Method: 基于Euclid广域巡天成像选源，结合Keck、HST、LBT和JWST多台望远镜光谱观测确认红移；利用JWST/NIRSpec G395M光谱测定系统红移，并通过H-beta和Mg II宽发射线宽度估算黑洞质量及爱丁顿比。

Result: 确认首个z > 8类星体J1504+6252（z = 8.129 ± 0.005），测得宽线区FWHM为~3160–3740 km/s，黑洞质量log10(MBH/Msun) = 8.33–8.89，爱丁顿比0.12–0.44，表明其为成熟、次爱丁顿吸积的类星体。

Conclusion: 该发现表明，宇宙早期已存在质量达数亿太阳质量的成熟黑洞，其形成需大质量种子或极高效率的持续增长；同时暗示z > 8可能存在更多高光度类星体，而此前已知再电离时期类星体可能只是更大质量黑洞群体中高速吸积的一端。

Abstract: Quasars powered by rapid accretion onto supermassive black holes (SMBHs) have been uncovered deep into the first gigayear of cosmic history. We present the discovery of EUCL J150452.291+625224.39 (J1504+6252), selected from Euclid Wide Survey imaging and spectroscopically confirmed as the first quasar at z > 8 through observations with Keck, HST, LBT, and JWST. J1504+6252 exhibits the defining spectral signatures of archetypal quasars at lower redshift, including a luminous blue power-law continuum and broad permitted emission lines, indicating a classical broad-line region. JWST/NIRSpec G395M spectroscopy yields a systemic redshift of z = 8.129 +/- 0.005, corresponding to 630 Myr after the Big Bang and shifting the quasar redshift frontier by about 39 Myr (6% of the age of the Universe at that epoch). From H-beta and Mg II, we measured intrinsic broad-line-region FWHM of 3160 +/- 270 and 3740 (+160/-170) km/s, respectively. A widely used single-epoch H-beta calibration gives a reference black hole mass of log10(MBH/Msun) = 8.56 +/- 0.43. Applying a range of calibrations to both Mg II and H-beta yields log10(MBH/Msun) = 8.33-8.89 and Eddington ratios of 0.12-0.44. Assembling a black hole of several hundred million solar masses within the first few hundred million years, despite its currently sub-Eddington accretion rate, requires either a massive seed or sustained, highly efficient early growth. The discovery of a mature, sub-Eddington quasar at z > 8 suggests that substantially more luminous quasars hosting similarly massive black holes may remain to be discovered. Conversely, previously known reionisation-era quasars may represent the rapidly accreting end of a broader population of already massive black holes spanning a wider range of accretion states.

</details>


### [116] [The hot circumgalactic medium: implications of mixing and resolution at the disk-halo interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31387)
*Omry Ginzburg,Jonathan Stern,Drummond B. Fielding,Claude-André Faucher-Giguère*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了星系盘-晕界面的微观物理过程对热CGM热力学结构和观测特征的影响，发现数值模拟中未解析该界面会导致人工混合，从而影响结果的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系盘-晕界面的微观物理过程在大多数CGM模拟中未被解析，但其可能显著影响热CGM的热力学结构与观测特征，因此需要深入研究。

Method: 通过理想化CGM模拟与FIRE zoom-in模拟对比，分析不同界面处理方式（如是否解析、是否引入内边界）对热CGM熵分布和X射线辐射的影响。

Result: 快速穿过界面（如湍流混合或喷泉流）导致近似等熵CGM；而由辐射冷却主导的界面则产生向外递增熵剖面。两者在旋转轴附近热气体密度和X射线发射测度上相差约10倍和100倍。未解析界面的模拟易得等熵结构，而施加慢流入内边界的模拟则再现向外递增熵剖面。

Conclusion: 数值模拟中未解析盘-晕界面会因人工混合导致错误的等熵CGM结构；真实CGM更可能具有向外递增熵剖面，这与eROSITA气泡观测推断一致。

Abstract: We demonstrate that microphysical processes at the disk--halo interface, typically unresolved in circumgalactic medium (CGM) simulations, strongly affect the thermodynamic structure and observational signatures of an inflowing hot ($\sim10^6\,{\rm K}$) CGM. If the hot gas is pulled through the interface faster than it can cool, e.g.\ by turbulent mixing with the ISM or fountain flows, the hot CGM approaches an isentropic state. If instead the interface is regulated by radiative cooling on the hot gas cooling time, the hot CGM develops an outward-increasing entropy profile akin to a classic cooling flow. The two regimes differ by a factor of $\sim\!10$ in the predicted hot gas density above the disk near the rotation axis, and consequently by a factor of $\sim100$ in the predicted X-ray emission measure. We show that an isentropic CGM is produced in idealized CGM simulations that fail to resolve the pc-scale disk--halo interface, as numerical mixing drives artificially rapid cooling at the interface. A similar isentropic structure is found along the rotation axis in FIRE zoom-in simulations of $z\sim0$ Milky-Way-mass galaxies, where the cooling layer is likewise unresolved, raising the possibility that numerical mixing contributes to the isentropic structure. In contrast, an outward-increasing entropy profile forms in our idealized simulations when we replace the unresolved interface with an inner boundary that enforces a slow inflow. This outward-increasing entropy profile is likely also present in the Milky Way hot CGM, inferred from comparing density estimates at $\sim100\,{\rm kpc}$ with the entropy of the eROSITA bubbles.

</details>


### [117] [The SDSS-V Black Hole Mapper Reverberation Mapping Project: Time-delayed Variability of a Quasar's C IV Broad Absorption Troughs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31415)
*Kaleb Roessler,Catherine J. Grier,Loren Robinson,W. N. Brandt,Patrick B. Hall,Yue Shen,Jonathan R. Trump,Keith Horne,William J. Nichols,Megan C. Davis,Adrian Amaya,Halima Mohamud,Lucas M. Seaton,Scott F. Anderson,Carolina Andonie,Dmitry Bizyaev,Mauricio Elias-Chavez,Xinyu Dai,Sean Morrison,Kaike Pan,Paola Rodriguez Hidalgo,Donald P. Schneider,Zachary Stone*

TL;DR: 本研究利用199条高信噪比光谱，追踪了红移z=3.228类星体SDSS J142129.40+522752.0中CIV吸收线的剧烈变化，发现两个吸收峰（Trough A和Trough B）在2022–2023年间短暂消失又重现，并表现出强相关性与约4天的滞后，表明电离状态变化及不同密度或距离的外流结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解活动星系核（AGN）中宽吸收线（BAL）类星体的结构、运动学和反馈机制。

Method: 分析SDSS III/IV 和 SDSS-V 项目在12年基线上获取的199条高信噪比光学光谱，量化两个CIV吸收特征（Trough A和Trough B）的等效宽度和平均深度的多年变异性，并研究其与g波段光度及其他吸收特征的相关性和时滞。

Result: Trough A和Trough B均表现出快速显著的等效宽度与深度变化，并在2022–2023年间短暂消失后重现；二者变化高度相关且与g波段光度相关；Trough B的等效宽度滞后Trough A约4天（静止参考系）。

Conclusion: 观测到的变异性主要由气体电离态变化驱动；Trough A与Trough B可能处于不同密度或距离的外流区域，反映了AGN外流的复杂分层结构。

Abstract: Broad absorption line (BAL) quasars offer a powerful view into the structure, kinematics, and feedback mechanics of active galactic nuclei. In this study, we track the dramatic behavior of intrinsic CIV outflows of the quasar SDSS J142129.40+522752.0 at z = 3.228. We analyze 199 high-signal- to-noise optical spectra collected over a 12-year baseline via the Sloan Digital Sky Survey III/IV Reverberation Mapping and the SDSS-V Black Hole Mapper Reverberation Mapping programs. We quantify multi-year variability across two prominent absorption features: a primary high-velocity CIV BAL trough (Trough A) and a lower-velocity mini-BAL (Trough B). Both troughs exhibit rapid, significant variations in equivalent width and average depth, culminating in temporary disappearances during the 2022-2023 observing seasons before reappearing at their original velocities. We find that the behavior of the two troughs is highly correlated with both the g-band flux and each other, strongly indicating that the observed variability is driven by ionization state changes of the gas. The equivalent width of the mini-BAL Trough B is found to lag that of Trough A by ~4 days in the quasar rest frame, suggesting that the equilibrium timescales of the two absorbers are different and possibly indicative of different outflow densities or distances.

</details>


### [118] [H$_3^+$ forbidden rotational lines in a planetary nebula: JWST/MIRI detection in the Ring Nebula and ionization by the central star](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31633)
*David A. Neufeld,Paule Sonnentrucker,Joel D. Green*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文报告了在环状星云（NGC 6720）中首次探测到H₃⁺的禁戒纯转动谱线，发现其发射与H₂发射空间一致，并需极高电离率，暗示动态过程（如光致蒸发前沿的分子气体输运）主导，而非静态光致电离模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H₃⁺是探测分子气体电离状态的关键探针，但此前未在行星状星云中被探测；本文旨在利用JWST数据检验高温恒星辐射对分子气体的电离机制。

Method: 分析JWST/MIRI在环状星云内环边缘获取的中红外光谱，识别并证认H₃⁺多个转动跃迁谱线；结合H₂转动温度成分（660 K与1700 K），构建统计平衡模型拟合H₃⁺谱线强度，并估算所需H₂电离率；对比静态光致电离模型与动态输运解释。

Result: 在16.325 μm处以32σ显著性探测到H₃⁺ (5,0)→(4,3)线及另外9条未混淆谱线；H₃⁺发射主要来自1700 K热H₂组分；所需H₂电离率（~3×10⁻¹² s⁻¹）比弥散云宇宙线电离率高约10⁴倍，可由中心135 kK恒星的EUV/软X射线辐射在特定柱密度（4–8×10¹⁹ cm⁻²）下提供；但静态模型无法产生足够高温H₂，支持动态输运机制。

Conclusion: H₃⁺的探测揭示行星状星云中分子气体受极端紫外辐射强烈电离，且其存在要求非静态、动力学过程（如H₂穿过光致蒸发前沿被加热），确立H₃⁺为研究高温恒星附近分子气体光致电离的新有效探针。

Abstract: We report the detection of the forbidden pure rotational spectrum of H$_3^+$ in the Ring Nebula (NGC 6720), to our knowledge the first detection of H$_3^+$ in a planetary nebula. In JWST/MIRI spectra obtained toward the inner edge of the main ring, 0.1 pc in projection from the central star, the $(J,K) = (5,0)\to(4,3)$ line at 16.325 $μ$m is detected at $32σ$, along with nine further unblended lines. The H$_3^+$ emission spatially follows the H$_2$ emission, whose rotational lines show warm (660 K) and hot (1700 K) components. A statistical-equilibrium model at these temperatures reproduces the subthermal H$_3^+$ line pattern, with much of the emission arising in the hot H$_2$. The emission requires an H$_2$ ionization rate of $\sim 3\times 10^{-12}\,{\rm s^{-1}}$ in the hot H$_2$ for an electron fraction of $2\times 10^{-4}$. The rate is $\sim 2\times 10^4$ times the cosmic-ray ionization rate in diffuse clouds. The extreme-ultraviolet and soft X-ray radiation (54-300 eV) of the 135 kK central star supplies this rate if the hot H$_2$ lies within an H-nucleus column density of (4-8)$\times 10^{19}\,{\rm cm^{-2}}$ of the ionization front of the dense globules. Static photoionization models, however, place the H$_2$ in gas too cold to excite the H$_3^+$ lines. The warm molecular gas and its H$_3^+$ thus suggest dynamical effects, most plausibly the advection of H$_2$ through a photoevaporation front into gas heated by the star. H$_3^+$ provides a new probe of the photoionization of molecular gas by very hot stars.

</details>


### [119] [Clumping Factor in the Mix: Incomplete Mixing Suppresses Turbulent Reaction Boost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31785)
*Yu-hsuan Liu,Chia-Yu Hu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过流体动力学模拟研究了超音速湍流中化学反应的亚网格处理方法，提出了一个考虑间歇性和混合效率的修正公式，以提高宇宙学模拟中未解析化学反应的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证宇宙学模拟中使用亚网格团块因子建模化学反应的合理性，并改进其在高马赫数湍流下的适用性。

Method: 对等温、超音速湍流进行流体动力学模拟，追踪一阶、二阶和三阶被动化学物种演化；在不同马赫数（M=2–50）和三种湍流驱动模式下，分析团块因子饱和行为及Damköhler数（Da）对混合效率ξ的影响；提出基于M和Da的拟合公式。

Result: 发现当M≳10时团块因子因间歇性而饱和；混合效率ξ显著依赖于Da：Da≪1时ξ→1（充分混合），Da≫1时ξ下降数倍至一个数量级以上；给出了ξ关于M和Da的普适拟合公式。

Conclusion: 提出的混合效率修正公式可有效提升宇宙学模拟中未解析化学反应的建模精度，尤其适用于高马赫数湍流环境。

Abstract: Cosmological simulations often account for unresolved density fluctuations using a sub-grid clumping factor when modeling chemical reactions. To validate this approach, we conduct hydrodynamical simulations of isothermal, supersonic turbulence and track the evolution of passive chemical species in first-, second-, and third-order reactions, exploring seven reaction rates across Mach numbers $M$ = 2-50 in three turbulence driving modes. We parametrize the saturation of clumping factors at $M \gtrsim 10$ due to intermittency (i.e., falling significantly below the log-normal prediction) with a one-parameter fitting formula. Furthermore, we find that the competition between turbulent mixing and the density-dependent reaction rates, governed by the Damköhler number (Da), suppresses the global reaction rate by a \textit{mixing efficiency} factor ($ξ$). For Da $\ll 1$, chemical species remain well mixed ($ξ\to 1$), and the reaction rates are boosted by a clumping factor. For Da $\gg 1$, reactants in dense gas are rapidly depleted before being redistributed by turbulence, causing $ξ$ to drop by a factor of a few to more than an order of magnitude. We quantify the effective mixing efficiency as a function of two dimensionless parameters, $M$ and Da, and provide a fitting formula that can be easily adopted in cosmological simulations to accurately follow unresolved chemical reactions.

</details>


### [120] [Electron processing of glycine: evidence for peptide-forming condensation reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32173)
*A. T. Hopkinson,D. V. Mifsud,P. Herczku,Z. Juhász,B. Sulik,N. J. Mason,N. C. Jones,S. V. Hoffmann,A. Cassidy,L. Hornekær,S. Ioppolo*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究通过电子辐照实验，证实甘氨酸在模拟星际环境条件下可发生缩合反应形成肽类物质，并产生水等副产物，表明高能辐射驱动的肽形成化学可能广泛存在于各类天体环境中，为地外生命前分子的起源与输送提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证肽类物质的形成是否是高能辐射处理的普遍结果，以支持氨基酸等有机分子可在多种地外环境中演化为更复杂生命前分子的假说。

Method: 在超高真空环境中制备甘氨酸及氘代甘氨酸（glycine-d3）薄膜，用1–2 keV电子辐照，并结合红外光谱、程序升温脱附（TPD）和真空紫外光谱（VUV）进行表征。

Result: 辐照后检测到H2O、HDO和D2O生成，证实发生脱水缩合反应；VUV谱显示CO、CO2等分解产物；残留非挥发性物质暗示肽类形成；电子与质子辐照结果相似，表明反应对辐射类型不敏感。

Conclusion: 高能电子辐照可有效驱动甘氨酸形成肽类，该过程具有普适性，支持复杂有机分子可在星际介质、小行星、彗星等多种天体环境中自然合成，并可能通过天体撞击输送到行星表面，参与生命起源。

Abstract: The discovery of amino acids on asteroids, including samples returned from asteroid Bennu, and the detection of glycine on comet 67P/Churyumov-Gerasimenko support the hypothesis that glycine can form in extraterrestrial environments. Laboratory experiments show that glycine can form through both energetic and non-energetic processing of interstellar ice analogues. In the interstellar medium, glycine is exposed to various forms of energetic processing, including cosmic rays and the secondary electrons they generate, which have been shown to produce complex organic molecules, including peptides. This work investigates whether peptide-forming chemistry is a general consequence of energetic processing. Films of glycine and glycine-d3 were grown in two ultrahigh-vacuum chambers and irradiated with 1-2 keV electrons. The irradiated samples were subsequently characterised using infrared spectroscopy, temperature-programmed desorption, and vacuum-ultraviolet spectroscopy. Peptide formation through condensation reactions would produce water as a secondary product, and spectra obtained following irradiation of glycine-d3 showed evidence of D2O formation, supported by TPD measurements, which revealed H2O formation for glycine and H2O, HDO, and D2O for glycine-d3. VUV spectra of irradiated glycine showed the formation of decomposition products, including CO and CO2, as well as H2O. Following heating, a non-volatile residue remained on the surface. Electron irradiation of glycine produces reaction products similar to proton irradiation, suggesting that the resulting chemistry is not strongly dependent on the energy source. These findings support the possibility that larger organic molecules can form in a wide range of extraterrestrial environments. Such molecules may subsequently be incorporated into small bodies and delivered to planetary surfaces, potentially contributing to the emergence of life.

</details>


### [121] [The ALMA REBELS Survey: multi-band dust continuum observations reveal heavily obscured starbursts among UV-bright galaxies at $z\sim7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32342)
*Hiddo Algera,Lucy Astles,Hanae Inami,Manuel Aravena,Tom Bakx,Rychard Bouwens,Rebecca Bowler,Karin Cescon,Chian-Chou Chen,Elisabete da Cunha,Claudia Di Cesare,Andreas Faisst,Andrea Ferrara,Rebecca Fisher,Yoshinobu Fudamoto,Luca Graziani,Ikki Mitsuhashi,Shoichiro Mizukoshi,Themiya Nanayakkara,Katherine Ormerod,Lucie Rowland,Raffaella Schneider,Sander Schouws,Renske Smit,Laura Sommovigo,Mauro Stefanon,Aswin Vijayan,Paul van der Werf,Joris Witstok*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用ALMA对13个红移z≈6.5–7.7的高红移星系进行多波段尘埃观测，发现其尘埃温度为30–70 K，部分为ULIRGs，红外光度主导（>90% SFR被遮蔽），尘埃质量比高（Mdust/Mstar ≈ 0.4%），表明再电离时期已存在富尘、高红外光度、强遮蔽的星系种群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙再电离时期（z > 6.5）星系中尘埃的起源、性质与演化，检验早期星系是否已具备显著尘埃含量及红外辐射特征，并探索其与后期大质量宁静星系的演化联系。

Method: 基于REBELS大型巡天及7个后续项目获取的ALMA多波段（含Band 8）连续谱数据，对13个z≈6.5–7.7的明亮星系开展统一的光学薄修正黑体拟合，推导尘埃质量、温度和红外光度。

Result: 尘埃温度T_dust ≈ 30–70 K（堆叠平均49^{+11}_{-8} K）；3个源为ULIRGs（L_IR > 10^12 L⊙），最高SFR_UV+IR达~300 M⊙/yr（位于z=7.67的REBELS-18）；红外遮蔽占比>90%，整体平均f_obs = 0.75±0.11；尘埃-恒星质量比Mdust/Mstar = 0.4^{+0.3}_{-0.2}%。

Conclusion: 在再电离时期（z∼7）已存在富尘、高红外光度、强遮蔽的星系种群；其恒星形成历史与z≈4–5的大质量宁静星系一致，是其合理前身；高尘埃质量比支持尘埃主要在ISM中高效生长的模型。

Abstract: Detailed studies with the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) have shown that dust is widespread even in UV-luminous galaxies at $z\gtrsim6.5$. Multi-band continuum observations are critical to fully characterize these early dust reservoirs. We present a homogeneous analysis of multi-band ALMA observations from the REBELS Large Program and seven follow-up programs, targeting thirteen massive galaxies at $6.5\lesssim z\lesssim7.7$ in at least two bands, including Band 8. Through optically thin modified blackbody fitting, we infer the dust masses, temperatures, and infrared luminosities of our targets. Their dust temperatures span $T_\mathrm{dust}\approx30-70\,\mathrm{K}$, and a stack reveals an average $T_\mathrm{dust}=49_{-8}^{+11}\,\mathrm{K}$. Three sources nominally qualify as ultra-luminous infrared galaxies (ULIRGs; $L_\mathrm{IR}>10^{12}\,L_\odot$), with total star formation rates of $\mathrm{SFR}_\mathrm{UV+IR}\approx160-300\,M_\odot\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$, the highest of which is found in our most distant target, REBELS-18 at $z=7.67$. Their star formation is heavily obscured, with $\gtrsim90\%$ emerging in the infrared, compared to an already large average obscured fraction of $f_\mathrm{obs}=0.75\pm0.11$ for the stack. These SFRs are consistent with the reconstructed growth histories of some massive quiescent galaxies at $z\approx4-5$, suggesting that dusty yet UV-bright galaxies at $z\sim7$ are plausible progenitors of such systems. The sample moreover displays large dust-to-stellar mass ratios ($M_\mathrm{dust}/M_\star = 0.4_{-0.2}^{+0.3}\%$), best reproduced by models in which dust grows efficiently in the interstellar medium. This demonstrates that a dust-rich, IR-luminous, and heavily obscured galaxy population is already in place well into the Epoch of Reionization.

</details>


### [122] [The Zero Enrichment Rare Objects (ZERO) Survey I: Photometric Calibration of NB395 and Metallicity Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32613)
*Bowen Huang,Nozomu Tominaga,Masashi Chiba,Haibo Yuan,Kai Xiao,Yutaka Komiyama,Miho N. Ishigaki,Rosemary F. G. Wyse,Evan N. Kirby,Carrie Filion,Kohei Hayashi,Tomoaki Ishiyama,Mikito Tanaka,Hajime Susa,L{á}szl{ó} Dobos,Alexander S. Szalay,Tam{á}s Budav{á}ri,Viska Wei,Itsuki Ogami,Yoshihisa Suzuki*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本论文介绍了ZERO巡天的首批成果，该巡天利用Subaru望远镜的Hyper Suprime-Cam开展窄带光度测量，旨在探测金属丰度敏感特征（NB395波段），实现对恒星钙丰度[Ca/H]的高精度估计，并构建包含百万源的光度与金属丰度星表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为深入研究银河系化学演化与结构，需大样本、高精度的恒星金属丰度测量；传统宽波段或其它窄带（如Ca H&K）巡天深度与精度不足，因此开展针对[Ca/H]敏感的NB395窄带巡天。

Method: 采用Subaru/HSC进行NB395窄带成像；利用校正后的Gaia BP/RP光谱和重定标的SDSS Stripe 82数据完成光度定标；提出基于物理约束的恒星主序拟合法（Physics-constrained Stellar Locus Fitting）估计[Ca/H]；通过多历元观测评估光度精度。

Result: 获得约35 deg²天区、10σ极限星等NB395≈24.5 mag的数据；光度零点精度~0.3%，平场精度优于1%；NB395光度精度达1.1%（亮于12等）、1.5%（20等）、10%（24等）；[Ca/H]精度优于0.18 dex（NB395<20），覆盖−3 ≤ [Ca/H] ≤ +0.5；确认NB395比[Fe/H]更直接示踪[Ca/H]；发布含100万源的星表，其中6.1万有[Ca/H]估计。

Conclusion: ZERO巡天首批数据在观测深度、光度定标精度及[Ca/H]估计可靠性方面均取得突破，为后续银河系化学-动力学联合研究及更大规模巡天奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We present the first results of the Zero Enrichment Rare Objects (ZERO) survey, a narrow-band photometric survey targeting metallicity-sensitive narrow bands using the Hyper Suprime-Cam on the Subaru Telescope. This paper outlines the scientific goals, survey strategy, current observational status, photometric calibration, and metallicity estimation. The current ZERO footprint comprises 21 pointings, covering approximately 35 deg$^2$ along the celestial equator in the NB395 band, reaching NB395 $\sim$ 24.5 mag (10$σ$) in one hour, significantly deeper than typical comparable narrow-band Ca H\&K surveys. The NB395 photometry is calibrated using corrected Gaia BP/RP spectra and the recalibrated SDSS Stripe 82 catalog, achieving a typical zero-point precision of $\sim$0.3 per cent and flat-field precision better than 1 per cent on spatial scales larger than 8$^\prime$. Under typical conditions (three repeats; total exposure time $\sim$1 hour), the photometric precision reaches $\sim$1.1 per cent at NB395 $\approx$ 12, $\sim$1.5 per cent at 20, and $\sim$10 per cent at 24. We estimate [Ca/H] using a Physics-constrained Stellar Locus Fitting Method. The resulting [Ca/H] precision is better than 0.18 dex for sources brighter than NB395 $\sim$ 20, spanning a wide metallicity range from metal-rich to the very metal-poor regime ($-3 \le$ [Ca/H]$\le +0.5$). We further show that NB395 traces [Ca/H] more directly than [Fe/H]. The final catalog contains one million sources with NB395 photometry, including 379,216 repeatedly observed and 61,845 with [Ca/H] estimates. These results provide a solid foundation for future ZERO survey, including photometric calibration, stellar parameter determination, and investigations of Galactic structure and chemical evolution.

</details>


### [123] [The dust attenuation scaling relation in the TNG50 simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32592)
*Pedro Cataldi,XianZhong Zheng,Antonios Katsianis,Sebastian Schulz,Man Qiao,Jianbo Qin,Zongfei Lyu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过使用SKIRT辐射传输代码后处理TNG50模拟中的星系，检验其红外超量（IRX）关系是否与观测一致，并分析偏差来源。结果发现TNG50在z=0.5–1时吻合良好，但在z=0偏高、z≥2偏低；偏差部分源于金属丰度、尘埃-金属比及尘埃成分设定，低红移偏差还需改进尘埃模型和观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证TNG50模拟中星系的红外超量（IRX）关系是否复现观测到的普适规律，并定位导致偏差的物理机制。

Method: 对TNG50中7280个恒星形成星系进行SKIRT辐射传输后处理，系统比较其IRX与金属丰度、红外光度、星系尺寸和倾角的关系；测试不同尘埃-金属比（DTM）和尘埃成分，校准星系性质，并分解各参数对偏差的贡献。

Result: TNG50星系在z=0.5–1与观测IRX关系高度一致；z=0时IRX偏高0.5 dex（主要因L_IR偏低需外推），z≥2时偏低0.25 dex（主要因金属丰度过高）；改变DTM或尘埃类型仅调整偏差幅度，无法消除红移演化趋势；z≥2偏差可通过降低金属丰度~0.2 dex显著缓解。

Conclusion: TNG50基本再现了IRX关系的功能形式，但存在系统性红移依赖偏差；低红移偏差需更完善的尘埃模型及对红外暗弱星系的观测约束，高红移偏差则提示模拟中金属丰度或DTM演化可能需修正。

Abstract: The infrared excess (IRX = L_IR / L_UV) of star-forming galaxies (SFGs) follows a tight scaling relation with star formation rate, gas-phase metallicity, and size up to z ~ 3, providing a key benchmark for galaxy formation models. We test whether TNG50 SFGs, post-processed with the radiative transfer code SKIRT, reproduce the universal IRX relation - linking IRX to metallicity, infrared luminosity, size, and inclination - and identify the origin of any offsets. We analyse 7280 TNG50-SFGs across 0 <= z <= 4, comparing their predicted IRX relations with observations and the EAGLE simulation. We test different dust-to-metal (DTM) ratios and dust compositions, recalibrate galaxy properties to observed scaling relations, and decompose the offsets into individual parameter contributions. TNG50-SFGs match the universal IRX relation closely at 0.5 <= z <= 1, but lie above it at z = 0 (+0.5 dex) and below it at z >= 2 (-0.25 dex). They follow a relation of the same functional form, with a shallower, metallicity-independent L_IR slope. At z ~ 0, TNG50-SFGs are ~1 dex fainter in L_IR than the local calibration sample, requiring extrapolation; at fixed stellar mass and L_IR, simulated and observed IRX agree within 0.04 dex. At z >= 2, TNG50-SFGs are less obscured than predicted, an offset largely removed by lowering their metallicities by ~0.2 dex. Varying the DTM ratio and dust type shifts the offset normalisation without resolving its redshift evolution: a redshift-dependent DTM ratio helps at high z, but not at z = 0. Resolving the low-z discrepancy requires both improved dust models and observational constraints on IR-faint galaxies.

</details>

### [124] [Free hyperboloidal evolution in 3D: gauge waves and constraint-violating gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31379)
*Alex Vañó-Viñuales*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次在三维数值相对论中实现了自由的双曲面演化，扩展了球对称下的共形紧化方法（BSSN/Z4），并成功在未来的零无穷远处非线性地演化了规范波和约束破坏引力波，验证了实现的可靠性与信号提取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在数值相对论模拟中直接到达未来零无穷远（future null infinity），从而更准确地提取引力波信号，需要发展适用于三维的双曲面演化方法。

Method: 将球对称下已有的共形紧化方法（基于BSSN/Z4形式）推广至全三维数值模拟，并采用自由演化方案进行双曲面演化。

Result: 成功实现了三维中非线性演化规范波和约束破坏引力波；验证了代码的可靠性，并能在未来零无穷远处成功提取物理信号。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个基于通用广义相对论公式的三维自由双曲面数值演化实现，为今后更复杂的天体物理场景（如黑洞并合）在零无穷远处直接提取引力波奠定了基础。

Abstract: Hyperboloidal evolution allows to reach future null infinity within numerical relativity simulations. This work extends the conformal compactification approach with BSSN/Z4 from spherical symmetry to full 3D simulations. This is the first free numerical hyperboloidal evolution in 3D with a common formulation of general relativity. In this first step, gauge waves and constraint-violating gravitational waves perturbing Minkowski spacetime are evolved nonlinearly and show that the implementation is reliable and signals can be successfully extracted at future null infinity.

</details>


### [125] [How to Play a Black Hole: Understanding Quasinormal Mode Excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31381)
*Lucía Vélez-Tartajo,Vitor Cardoso,Jaime Redondo-Yuste*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了施瓦西黑洞准正规模（QNM）在受控探针下的激发机制，发现初始数据的宽度和位置、下落源的动力学以及强场区域的贡献显著影响不同模式的相对激发强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞准正规模的激发机制对解释引力波信号及推断黑洞性质至关重要。

Method: 通过数值模拟和理论分析，研究不同初始数据分布、下落粒子源以及源截断半径对施瓦西黑洞准正规模激发的影响。

Result: 宽初始数据抑制模式激发、增强晚期尾项；近光球局域数据抑制泛音模；下落源引发两个阶段环-down；强场外关源导致波形指数级接近标准下落波形。

Conclusion: QNM激发强烈依赖于扰动的性质与动力学，且主要源自强场区域，特别是光球附近；该工作为研究黑洞环-down起源提供了可控框架。

Abstract: Understanding the excitation of black hole quasinormal modes is essential for interpreting gravitational-wave signals and inferring black hole properties from them. In this work, we investigate the excitation of the quasinormal modes of Schwarzschild black holes under controlled probes. In the absence of a source, we find that broader initial data suppresses mode excitation, enhancing the relative contribution of the late-time tail, while data localized near the light ring strongly suppresses overtones relative to the fundamental mode. On the other hand, plunging sources give rise to two distinct ringdown stages, associated with scattered radiation generated during the particle's infall and with the particle crossing the light ring. Finally, we investigate how much of the signal is sourced from the strong-field region by switching off the source at some finite radius. The resulting waveform is exponentially close -- in cutoff radius -- to that of a standard infall. These results provide insight into the dependence of QNM excitation on the properties and dynamics of the perturbation, and offer a controlled setting in which to investigate the origin of black hole ringdown.

</details>


### [126] [On the evidence of lensing in GW231123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31389)
*Anna Liu,Mick Wright,Jef Heynen,Eungwang Seo,Isaac C. F. Wong,Disha Hegde,Laura E. Uronen,Harsh Narola,Chris Van Den Broeck,Martin Hendry,Tjonnie G. F. Li,Otto A. Hannuksela,Justin Janquart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了引力波事件GW231123是否可能由引力透镜效应引起，发现透镜解释能缓解不同波形模型间对自旋和质量推断的差异，并给出约3.96σ的统计显著性，虽具说服力但尚不足以确证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW231123是迄今观测到质量最大的双黑洞并合事件，其高推断质量和自旋参数对波形模型高度敏感，作者旨在检验引力透镜是否可解释这些异常特征。

Method: 将信号建模为同一并合事件多个重叠透镜像的叠加，并在四种波形模型下进行贝叶斯分析，同时与无透镜背景信号对比评估显著性。

Result: 透镜解释在四种波形模型下均获支持，降低了高自旋推断的置信度，使不同模型下的质量和自旋推断更一致；透镜假设的统计显著性为3.96^{+0.68}_{-0.30}σ。

Conclusion: 引力透镜是解释GW231123观测特征的一个有吸引力且自洽的假说，但当前显著性尚未达到确证水平（通常需5σ）。

Abstract: Just like light, gravitational waves may be gravitationally lensed by massive objects along their travel path, however, this has not been observed so far. GW231123 is the most massive binary black-hole merger observed to-date, and quasi-circular gravitational wave analyses infer high masses and spin parameters that are particularly sensitive to the waveform model used. Here we investigate whether gravitational lensing can account for these features. We model the signal as a superposition of multiple overlapping images of the same merger and find support for lensing across four waveform models. The lensing interpretation reduces the support for these high spins, bringing both the inferred spins and masses into closer agreement across waveform models. A comparison with a background of unlensed signals gives the lensing interpretation an estimated significance of $3.96^{+0.68}_{-0.30}σ$. This makes lensing a compelling interpretation of this event but is insufficient for a definitive claim.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum black hole evaporation from thin shell collapse: a complete effective picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31399)
*Michał Bobula,Francesco Fazzini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受圈量子引力启发的有效模型，统一描述薄壳坍缩、黑洞形成、蒸发及残余物动力学，发现不同蒸发定律下黑洞最终状态存在显著差异：Stefan-Boltzmann律导致带事件与柯西视界的极值黑洞，而Schwinger机制则产生无视界、全局双曲且逃逸至未来零无穷的类光残余物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典奇点问题并提供完整可预测的时空图像，特别是黑洞演化全过程（形成、蒸发、残余）的量子引力刻画。

Method: 构建基于圈量子引力的通用有效模型，引入准静态近似下的回响效应，将辐射建模为零尘，并利用Darmois-Israel拼接条件匹配蒸发面上的入射与出射通量。

Result: 在Stefan-Boltzmann蒸发律下得到具事件与柯西视界的极值黑洞并延伸至新渐近区；在Schwinger机制下不形成任何视界，仅出现暂态动力学囚禁区，最终生成逃逸至未来零无穷的无视界类光残余物，且时空保持全局双曲。

Conclusion: 量子引力效应对黑洞终态具有决定性影响，蒸发机制的选择直接导致时空结构（如视界、因果性）的根本差异，支持黑洞信息悖论可能通过无视界残余物得以缓解。

Abstract: Quantum gravity is expected to play a central role in resolving classical singularities and providing a complete, predictive spacetime picture. In this paper, we develop an effective model inspired by loop quantum gravity that aims to fulfill this expectation, providing a unified description of thin shell collapse, black hole formation, evaporation, and remnant dynamics. The framework exhibits general validity, holding across different effective equations of motion, matter sources, and evaporation laws. Specifically, we incorporate backreaction within a quasi-static approximation, modeling the radiation as null dust and matching the ingoing and outgoing fluxes across an evaporation surface using the Darmois-Israel junction conditions. For an evaporation law governed by the Stefan-Boltzmann law, we find that the final stage of evaporation yields an extremal black hole extending into a new asymptotic region, featuring both event and Cauchy horizons. Conversely, under the Schwinger approach, no event or Cauchy horizons form, instead, a temporary dynamical trapped region emerges, and the resulting spacetime remains globally hyperbolic. Under this variant, our model predicts a horizonless null remnant escaping to future null infinity.

</details>


### [128] [Split Ends: UV Physics branches Black Hole Hair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31438)
*Georg Trenkler,Leonardo G. Trombetta,Alexander Vikman*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标量场作为戈德斯通模如何通过耦合高斯-博内特项影响黑洞结构，并理解质量间隙尺度下对称性恢复效应对黑洞性质的影响。

Method: 将平移对称标量场建模为复标量场的戈德斯通模，引入对称性破缺势；分析该有质量标量场在黑洞背景（尤其当黑洞尺寸与质量间隙倒数可比时）下的动力学行为及其对黑洞几何（如视界面积、表面引力）的影响。

Result: 发现此类黑洞存在多解性：相同质量下可对应最多三种不同视界面积和表面引力；且在质量间隙尺度附近，对称性恢复效应显著增强黑洞与广义相对论预测的偏差。

Conclusion: 该模型揭示了黑洞物理中新的分支结构和可观测效应，为通过引力波探测新物理（如轻标量场及对称性破缺机制）提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Coupling a shift-symmetric scalar field to the Gauss-Bonnet invariant modifies black holes (BHs) via scalar hair. Here we consider this field to be the Goldstone of a complex scalar field with a symmetry-breaking potential. For BHs of a size comparable with the inverse mass gap, the gapped field tries to restore the symmetry close to the horizon, enhancing the departure from standard BHs. Moreover, BHs of the same mass can have up to three different horizon areas and surface gravities. This branching has profound consequences for BH physics, potentially observable in gravitational waves.

</details>


### [129] [Anisotropic Fluid Inhomogeneous Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31461)
*Mariano Cadoni,Andrea Cozzumbo,Riccardo Murgia,Matteo Pili,Mirko Pitzalis,Andrea P. Sanna,Matteo Schulz,Ilaria Usai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义相对论与各向异性流体源的非均匀宇宙学新框架（AFIC），通过一个输入函数（不同红移下的径向Misner-Sharp质量分布）实现爱因斯坦方程的精确解，能自然解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀，并为DESI观测结果提供物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服标准微扰理论在非线性结构形成阶段的局限性，寻找无需引入暗能量即可解释晚期宇宙加速的纯引力机制。

Method: 构建以各向异性流体为源、以Misner-Sharp质量分布为参数的精确广义相对论模型（AFIC），分析其大尺度动力学行为与FLRW极限。

Result: 模型在早期和大尺度晚期恢复FLRW宇宙学；发现由物质不均匀性诱导的纯大尺度效应可驱动晚期加速，该效应不改变局域引力势；给出当前有效暗能量密度的解析估计。

Conclusion: AFIC为晚期加速提供一种几何/引力起源的物理解释，表明观测到的‘暗能量’可能源于大尺度结构的非均匀性，而非新组分或修正引力。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for inhomogeneous cosmology based on General Relativity sourced by an anisotropic fluid. The model is parametrized by a single input function, the radial Misner--Sharp mass distribution at different redshifts, and admits exact solutions to Einstein's equations. Going beyond the standard assumption of small metric perturbations, it provides a fully relativistic framework to describe the non-linear regime of cosmic structure formation. The Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) cosmology is recovered both at early times and -- on large scales only -- at late times. Our Anisotropic Fluid Inhomogeneous Cosmology (AFIC) model provides a natural mechanism through which matter inhomogeneities give rise to late-time acceleration. The extensive term driving this effect does not enter the exact metric solution and therefore does not contribute to the local gravitational potential. Rather, it emerges as a genuinely large-scale effect induced by the inhomogeneous distribution of matter at late times. This mechanism may provide a physical interpretation of the results from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI). Finally, we present a simple analytical realization of the model and use it to provide an estimate of the present-day effective dark energy density.

</details>


### [130] [Prospects for Detecting Post-merger Signals from Binary Neutron Stars with Future Gravitational-Wave Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31857)
*Jingwang Diao,Teng Zhang,Xingjiang Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文比较了通过随机引力波背景（SGWB）和直接探测单个并合后信号两种方式，研究双中子星并合后阶段的可行性，发现宽带SGWB可被第三代探测器探测到，而千赫兹频段SGWB仍低于阈值；单事件PM波段探测在特定模型下每年可得数至百余候选体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双中子星并合后的相位携带致密物质状态方程与并合残余物信息，但其观测前景尚不明确，需评估不同探测路径的有效性。

Method: 采用统一的‘旋进–并合–并合后’（IPM）波形模型和相同天体物理种群模型，对比随机引力波背景（SGWB）与单事件直接探测，并评估ET、CE及专用千赫兹探测器等第三代探测器配置下的灵敏度。

Result: 宽带BNS SGWB在所考虑的探测器网络下超过检测阈值；千赫兹频段SGWB未达阈值；专用千赫兹探测器每年可探测约5–174个PM波段候选体（基线IPM模型），数值相对论情形下为0.07–7个/年。

Conclusion: 宽带SGWB是当前更可行的并合后整体统计探针，而高灵敏度千赫兹专用探测器对单事件并合后信号探测具有重要潜力，尤其在改进波形建模后。

Abstract: The post-merger (PM) phase of binary neutron star (BNS) mergers carries information about the merger remnant and the dense-matter equation of state, but its observational prospects remain uncertain. We compare two approaches to accessing this emission: the stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) from the BNS population and direct PM-band detections of individually resolved mergers. Using a unified inspiral--merger--post-merger (IPM) waveform prescription and the same astrophysical population model for both calculations, we evaluate representative third-generation detector configurations involving ET, CE, and a dedicated kilohertz detector. For the representative detector networks considered, the baseline IPM model predicts that the all-source, broadband BNS SGWB exceeds the adopted detection threshold across the local merger-rate interval. In contrast, the kilohertz PM-band SGWB remains below this threshold. For individual events, over $R_0=7.6$--$250~{\rm Gpc}^{-3}\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$, the baseline IPM model predicts approximately $5$--$174$ PM-band candidates per year for the dedicated kilohertz detector, whereas the numerical-relativity cases considered here yield approximately $0.07$--$7~{\rm yr}^{-1}$.

</details>


### [131] [Classical phase space distributions for quantum states in cosmology: General states, operator ordering and the geometry of fading non-commutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31912)
*Alejandro Corichi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文扩展了先前关于FLRW背景中线性场真空态相空间概率分布的工作，给出了任意量子态对应的经典相空间分布的显式公式，并分析了其非负性条件、算符排序差异、相关系数与 squeezing 的几何解释，以及在宇宙学背景（特别是de Sitter和辐射主导时期）中的具体行为与物理含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立量子场论中量子态与经典相空间概率分布之间的精确对应关系，尤其关注Weyl有序n点函数与分布矩的一致性，并推广至任意态与宇宙学背景，以深化半经典诠释和量子-经典过渡的理解。

Method: 采用Ashtekar变量框架，在Bargmann表象中构造量子态Ψ对应的相空间分布ρ_Ψ；利用Kähler几何刻画高斯态；通过Hamilton向量场夹角统一描述关联性、对易/反对易比与squeezing；结合FLRW与de Sitter时空的模式函数演化分析非对易修正的时序衰减行为。

Result: 1) 得到任意态ρ_Ψ的显式公式，纯态下仅相干态与squeezed态使ρ_Ψ非负；2) 任意线性观测量的算符排序差可由c数对易子显式表达；3) 纯高斯态中两观测量的经典相关系数、对易/反对易比及squeezing由其Hamilton向量场夹角统一决定；4) 在FLRW（尤其de Sitter与辐射时代）中，ρ_Ψ精确复现所有Weyl有序n点函数，非对易修正呈指数衰减，且squeezing导致±k模纠缠与贝尔不等式违背。

Conclusion: 相空间正定分布ρ_Ψ能精确刻画特定可观测量族（如线性、Weyl有序或经高斯平滑的量）的量子统计，但不能覆盖全部测量——其适用性受限于态的高斯性、可观测量的类型及时空背景，揭示了量子性（如squeezing、纠缠）与经典概率描述之间的根本张力。

Abstract: In earlier work with Ashtekar and Kesavan we showed that, for linear fields in any FLRW background, the vacuum defines a probability distribution $ρ_o$ on the classical phase space whose moments are exactly the Weyl-ordered $n$-point functions. We extend that analysis in four directions. First, for an arbitrary state $Ψ$ we give an explicit formula, in the Bargmann representation, for the distribution $ρ_Ψ$ that reproduces all Weyl-ordered expectation values. For pure states it is non-negative precisely for coherent and squeezed states, each the Gaussian of a compatible Kähler metric, and the Gaussian smoothing needed to make it positive is either zero or the full coherent-state width. Second, for linear observables every operator ordering differs from the Weyl ordering by explicit products of $c$-number commutators. Third, for two linear observables in a pure Gaussian state, the classical correlation coefficient, the ratio of commutator to anticommutator and the squeezing are functions of a single angle between their Hamiltonian vector fields; this bounds the departure of arbitrarily ordered $n$-point functions from the moments of $ρ$. Fourth, for fields in FLRW the equality holds at arbitrary, possibly different, times and for every homogeneous isotropic quasi-free state. In de Sitter the non-commutative corrections fall as $e^{-N}$ for field and momentum and as $e^{-3N}/3$ for the field at two times. For the state inherited from inflation they stay suppressed in the radiation era, by the inverse occupation number, except near the nodes of the mode functions. The same squeezing entangles the modes $\pm\vec k$ relative to the radiation-era vacuum and makes displaced-parity measurements violate Bell inequalities, so the positive distribution describes a specified family of observables, not all measurements.

</details>


### [132] [Machine learning enables full Bayesian inference for spinning extreme-mass-ratio inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31916)
*Bo Liang,Minghui Du,Zhenwei Lyu,Hanlin Song,Jiakai Zhang,Weichen Qin,Chia-Jui Chou,Yi Yang,Jingyi Yu,Ziren Luo,Mingming Sun,Peng Xu,Yue-Liang Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次从完整先验出发，成功实现了对14参数自旋极端质量比旋进（EMRI）源的联合参数推断，利用生成网络缩小先验范围并揭示自旋方向简并环，再结合精确探测器似然函数获得后验分布；在模拟LISA-Taiji一年观测数据上，黑洞红移质量与自旋精度达10^{-5}量级，小天体质量精度达10^{-4}，并实现0.8%距离测量与亚平方度定位，支持暗警铃宇宙学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EMRI参数推断大多基于已知源参数假设或简化模型，尚未解决从完整宽先验出发对全部14个参数（含自旋）的联合反演问题。

Method: 采用增强解析拼凑（augmented analytic kludge）模型；利用生成网络压缩完整先验空间、识别出自旋方向的简并环结构；再结合精确的探测器似然函数进行贝叶斯后验推断。

Result: 在模拟LISA-Taiji一年观测下，首次获得完整先验下的14参数自旋EMRI后验分布：中心黑洞红移质量与自旋精度达2×10^{-5}，小天体红移质量精度达10^{-4}，轨道为偏心、逆行；距离误差0.8%，天空定位精度亚平方度；实现辐射反应主导的长时相干分析。

Conclusion: 该方法突破了EMRI宽先验高维参数推断的技术瓶颈，显著提升强场引力波天体物理与宇宙学测量精度，为LISA等未来空间引力波任务提供关键分析范式。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs), stellar-mass compact objects spiralling into massive black holes, promise to measure the masses and spins of the central black holes, reveal the strong-field environment around them and gauge cosmic distances via millihertz gravitational-wave observations. Yet most forecasts for the parameter inference of these systems assume known source parameters, and broad-prior analyses have addressed only simplified versions of the problem. The joint inversion of all 14 parameters of a spinning source from the complete prior has remained unsolved. Here we solve it for the augmented analytic kludge model: generative networks narrow the complete prior and reveal a ring of degenerate spin directions, and the exact detector likelihood determines the posterior. For a simulated one-year LISA-Taiji observation, within one ring sector we obtain, for the first time, a 14-parameter posterior of a spinning EMRI from the complete prior, the most precise yet obtained for such a source from broad priors. The redshifted mass and spin of the massive black hole are determined to two parts in $10^5$, the mass two orders of magnitude beyond any electromagnetic measurement. The compact object's redshifted mass is measured to one part in $10^4$, and its eccentric, retrograde orbit discriminates between capture channels. The 0.8% distance and sub-square-degree localization are sufficient for dark-siren cosmology. Year-long coherent analysis resolves the inspiral driven by radiation reaction itself.

</details>


### [133] [Completing the real-space construction of the canonical harmonic coordinates of Kerr metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31994)
*Zizheng Xing,Xiaokai He,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在多极后闵可夫斯基（MPM）框架下，精确确定了所有后闵可夫斯基（PM）阶的规范谐波Kerr度规，并证明其不包含二阶及以上PM阶的拉普拉斯算子齐次分量，从而实现了零规范矩的归纳构造；该度规与Boyer-Lindquist坐标系通过一个复调和径向函数的实部所生成的坐标变换相联系，并得到独立工作的交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建严格满足谐波规范且具明确物理意义（如零非质量型多极矩）的Kerr黑洞度规，统一不同形式（如Boyer-Lindquist与MPM）之间的关系，并验证高阶PM展开的一致性。

Method: 基于多极后闵可夫斯基（MPM）形式主义，结合形式幂级数环分析，证明拉普拉斯算子齐次项在n≥2阶PM中消失；利用线性场与规范Kerr解一致的初始条件进行归纳构造；引入复调和径向函数的实部生成坐标变换；并与Sadu-Vanhove动量空间递归方法交叉比对。

Result: 获得全阶PM的闭式规范谐波Kerr度规；严格证明其不含n≥2阶拉普拉斯齐次分量；实现所有规范矩为零的归纳构造；给出到Boyer-Lindquist坐标的显式坐标变换；结果获独立方法交叉验证。

Conclusion: 规范谐波Kerr度规可在MPM框架下以闭式、无规范矩方式精确构造，其结构由基本调和性约束主导，强化了广义相对论中运动学与几何表述的内在一致性。

Abstract: In this paper, we determine the exact canonical harmonic Kerr metric to all post-Minkowskian (PM) orders within the multipolar post-Minkowskian (MPM) formalism. The metric agrees with the previously constructed 4PM results. Using a formal series ring, we prove that no homogeneous components of Laplacian operator appear in this metric at PM order $n\ge2$. Together with the equality of the linear field with the canonical Kerr solution, this property allows an inductive construction of the canonical MPM metric where all gauge moments vanish. The coordinate transformation linking the canonical harmonic Kerr metric to the Boyer-Lindquist metric is generated by the real part of a complex harmonic radial function. A recent independent construction by Sadu and Vanhove using momentum-space recursion and a residual homogeneous freedom classification yields the same result, providing a cross validation of the closed-form canonical harmonic Kerr metric.

</details>


### [134] [Analytic response functions of space-based gravitational-wave detectors to ultralight scalar-field dark matter under multiple types of TDI combinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32563)
*Wenqiu Tang,Pan-Pan Wang,Yuezhe Chen,Rui Luo,Cheng-Gang Shao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了空间引力波探测器对超轻标量场暗物质的响应函数解析表达式，涵盖多种时间延迟干涉（TDI）组合，并分析其低频行为，为LISA、太极、天琴等任务探测该类暗物质提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻标量场是重要的暗物质候选者，其相干振荡可能通过与测试质量耦合在空间引力波探测器中留下特征信号，需建立精确响应模型以提升探测灵敏度。

Method: 从标量场与测试质量的耦合作用出发，推导测试质量附加位移和单链多普勒响应；进而给出任意TDI组合下响应函数的一般解析形式，并通过蒙特卡洛数值模拟验证最优组合A、E、T的结果；最后按低频行为将TDI组合分为普通型和ζ型并给出各自低频响应表达式。

Result: 获得了多种TDI组合下超轻标量场暗物质响应函数的解析解，验证了其与数值结果的一致性，并完成了TDI组合的低频行为分类及对应低频响应表达式。

Conclusion: 所获解析响应函数可用于评估LISA、太极、天琴等空间引力波任务对超轻标量场暗物质质量及耦合参数的探测灵敏度，为未来数据分析和信号搜寻提供关键理论工具。

Abstract: Ultralight scalar fields are important candidates for dark matter. As coherently oscillating fields, they can leave characteristic signatures in space-based gravitational-wave detectors through their coupling to test masses. In this paper, we derive analytic expressions for the response functions of space-based gravitational-wave detectors to ultralight scalar-field dark matter under multiple types of time-delay interferometry (TDI) combinations. Starting from the coupling action between the scalar field and a test mass, we obtain the additional displacement of the test mass and the single-link Doppler response. We then give the general analytic form of the response function for an arbitrary TDI combination and compare the analytic and Monte Carlo numerical results for the optimal combinations A, E, and T. Finally, we analyze the responses of multiple types of TDI combinations, classify them into ordinary and ζ types according to their low-frequency behavior, and give the low-frequency response expressions of the individual combinations. The results can be used to evaluate the sensitivity of space-based gravitational-wave missions such as LISA, Taiji, and TianQin to the mass and coupling parameters of ultralight scalar-field dark matter.

</details>


### [135] [Exact Mass Multipoles of Distorted Schwarzschild Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32283)
*Brennen Black*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用孤立视界框架，精确计算了Weyl畸变Schwarzschild黑洞视界的几何与质量多极矩，未对畸变做线性近似；针对纯四极和偶极-四极-八极两种畸变情形给出闭式解，并分析了低阶近似带来的误差规律及奇宇称项的可忽略条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Weyl畸变下黑洞视界多极结构的精确特征，评估常用线性或低阶近似在计算多极矩时的系统误差。

Method: 基于孤立视界框架，从多极矩的精确积分表达式出发，对纯四极畸变情形求得任意ℓ阶的闭式解（以合流超几何函数表示）；对偶极-四极-八极畸变情形，构造关于奇宇称系数β的闭式级数解，其系数对四极系数α₂严格精确。

Result: 发现α₂主导的低阶近似对各无量纲质量多极矩引入约α₂量级的相对误差，且误差主要集中在线性化的四极矩而非被忽略的高阶多极；提出一个幂次计数规则，指出各阶畸变对多极矩的贡献阶数；推导出奇宇称项需小于α₂²才可忽略；截断β级数至首阶β项可在|β|,|α₂|≤1/8时使ℓ≤6多极矩精度达~10⁻³。

Conclusion: 低阶近似虽简便但存在系统性偏差，尤其在线性化四极矩中；精确多极结构依赖于畸变参数的非线性组合，奇宇称畸变不可轻易忽略；所获闭式解为高精度建模强场畸变黑洞提供了可靠基准。

Abstract: Using the isolated-horizon framework, we compute the geometric and mass multipole moments of the Weyl-distorted Schwarzschild horizon without linearizing the distortion. Starting from the exact integral representation we evaluate it in closed form for two cases, a pure quadrupole distortion and a dipole-quadrupole-octupole distortion. For a pure quadrupole we obtain a closed form for all $\ell$ in terms of confluent hypergeometric functions, while for a dipole-quadrupole-octupole distortion we derive a closed-form series solution in the odd-parity coefficient $β$ whose coefficients are exact in the quadrupole coefficient $α_{2}$. We use these results to understand the errors incurred by leading-order approximations. Leading-order approximations in $α_{2}$ misestimate every dimensionless mass multipole by a relative error of roughly $α_{2}$, nearly independent of $\ell$, and for pure linearization most of the error lies in the linearized quadrupole moment rather than the discarded higher multipoles. A simple degree-counting rule determines which orders of distortion contribute to each multipole. As a consequence, the odd-parity component must be small compared with $α_{2}^2$ before it can be neglected. Finally, truncating the series in $β$ at its first $β$-dependent order reproduces the $\ell \leq 6$ multipoles to about $10^{-3}$ for $|β|,|α_{2}| \leq 1/8$.

</details>


### [136] [Horizon dynamical entropies and phase space topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32444)
*Matteo Bruno,Simone Speziale*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由无限维范数诱导的相空间拓扑，以解决零超曲面微分同胚对应的哈密顿生成元的'可积性'问题，并旨在比较不同黑洞动力学熵的定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决零超曲面微分同胚对应的哈密顿生成元的'可积性'问题，并比较不同黑洞动力学熵的定义。

Method: 研究由无限维范数诱导的相空间拓扑，分析标量场和引力场的可积性条件，特别是利用Sobolev范数。

Result: 发现标量场存在一族范数可保证不同极化下的可积性；而引力场仅需该族中的一个成员（即文献中已考虑的Sobolev范数）即可满足可积性，其余候选范数因度规半正定性而被排除。

Conclusion: Sobolev范数是适用于引力场并确保哈密顿生成元可积性的关键范数，为黑洞动力学熵的比较提供了数学基础。

Abstract: We study phase space topologies induced by infinite-dimensional norms as a means to solve the problem of 'integrability' of Hamiltonian generators corresponding to diffeomorphisms of a null hypersurface, with the specific goal in mind to compare different prescriptions for the dynamical entropy of black holes, which replaces the area in non-stationary regimes. We first show that for a scalar field, there exists a family of norms that would provide integrability for different polarizations. We then show that a single member of this family applies to the gravitational field, and it is given by the Sobolev norm already considered in the literature. The obstruction to the other candidates lies in the semi-positivity of the metric.

</details>


### [137] [Toward Solving the Galactic Foreground Problem in Gravitational-Wave Astronomy with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32800)
*Bo Liang,Jinwen Deng,Minghui Du,Hanlin Song,Xilong Fan,Peng Xu,Ziren Luo,Lijing Shao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经流和贝叶斯推理的方法，用于在空间引力波探测器数据中精确计数和分辨银河系致密双星系统，并定义了由数据本身决定的‘贝叶斯混淆极限’，为弱信号叠加问题提供了普适性理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间引力波探测器将观测到数千万个银河系双星，但其分辨与未分辨状态对科学目标（如大质量黑洞并合、极端质量比旋进等）影响巨大；而双星数量在数学上是病态定义的，因信号振幅连续分布，需引入可观测定义。

Method: 采用神经流（neural flows）进行高效计数，并结合嵌套采样（nested sampling）实现高精度分辨，甚至可区分频率间隔小于一个频 bin 的成对信号；同时从贝叶斯信息论角度导出‘混淆极限’——信号需提供约20 nat的信息量才被视为可分辨源。

Result: 在0.5–10 mHz频段内，神经流计数误差控制在每1 μHz区间±1以内（99%置信度）；嵌套采样成功分辨密集双星；首次明确数据驱动的贝叶斯混淆极限，适用于射电巡天、脉冲星计时阵列及超分辨显微等广泛领域。

Conclusion: 该方法将银河系双星前景转化为具有解析选择函数的定量化源计数，为未来空间引力波探测器的全局建模、种群检验及多信使天体物理研究奠定基础；混淆极限揭示了弱信号分辨的根本性数据约束，超越具体算法局限。

Abstract: Space-borne gravitational-wave detectors will see tens of millions of Galactic binaries. Resolved, they would form a census of the Galaxy's ultracompact binaries unhindered by dust; unresolved, they are the dominant noise up to a few millihertz for massive black-hole binaries, extreme-mass-ratio inspirals and backgrounds from the early Universe. Separating the two requires the number of binaries, which is ill-defined because binaries exist at every amplitude. We make it an observable by defining a source as a binary above a signal-to-noise threshold. Neural flows count the binaries to within one in 99% of 1 microhertz intervals over 0.5-10 mHz, and nested sampling resolves them, even pairs closer than one frequency bin. The data themselves set a limit: a source is resolvable only if its signal pays for its own description, about 20 nats, which biases even ideal trans-dimensional samplers. Like the diffraction limit in imaging, this Bayesian confusion limit is set by the data rather than by the method, and it governs any superposition of weak sources, from radio surveys and pulsar timing arrays to super-resolution microscopy. For future space-borne detectors, the method turns the Galactic foreground into a definite, calibrated count of binaries with an analytic selection function, the basis for global fits and for tests of population models.

</details>


### [138] [An uncalibrated effective-one-body waveform model for intermediate-mass-ratio black hole binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32840)
*Angelica Albertini,Vojtěch Witzany*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对质量比q>10的非自旋准圆轨道双星系统的有效单体（EOB）波形模型，该模型仅基于解析后牛顿结果构建动力学，具有高精度和高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间质量比双星系统（质量比q~10^2-10^4）是波形建模中最具挑战性的源之一，亟需高精度且高效的波形模型。

Method: 构建了一个基于解析后牛顿理论的、适用于非自旋准圆轨道双星（q>10）的启发式有效单体（EOB）模型，并采用优化的后绝热实现方法以加速波形生成。

Result: 该模型在与数值相对论模拟对比时，(2,2)模式的不忠实度达10^{-4}-10^{-5}；在q=10^4-10^5时，与二阶自力波形相比，四年旋进过程中的相位误差小于1弧度；波形生成速度比完整运动方程积分快两个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提出的EOB模型在宽广质量比范围内兼具高精度与高效率，为引力波数据分析提供了强有力的新工具。

Abstract: Intermediate-mass-ratio binaries, with mass ratios $q=m_1/m_2\sim10^2-10^4$, are among the most challenging sources for waveform modeling. We present an inspiral-merger-ringdown effective-one-body model for nonspinning, quasicircular binaries with $q>10$, with dynamics built only from analytical post-Newtonian results. It reaches $(2,2)$ unfaithfulness of $10^{-4}-10^{-5}$ against numerical-relativity simulations with $q>10$ and subradian dephasing against second-order self-force waveforms over four-year inspirals at $q=10^4-10^5$. An optimized post-adiabatic implementation enables waveform generation up to two orders of magnitude faster than integrating the full equations of motion.

</details>


### [139] [Strong hyperbolicity of puncture-gauge formulations of gravitational theories with second order equations of motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32853)
*Shunhui Yao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了在弱耦合条件下，修正的CCZ4（mCCZ4）公式在(d+1)维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力和四维四阶导数量子-张量理论中具有强双曲性，前提是规范参数满足锥分离条件；该结论通过将mCCZ4方程重写为类似Kovács-Reall修正调和公式的二阶系统，并采用其协变特征分析与连续性论证得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为确保高阶引力理论数值模拟的稳定性与适定性，需验证其演化方程组是否具备强双曲性，而此前相关分析多局限于爱因斯坦极限下的一阶微扰，缺乏对全主符号的严格处理。

Method: 将mCCZ4方程重写为形式等价的二阶系统，使其导数结构类比Kovács-Reall修正调和公式；在此基础上，直接应用其协变特征分析与连续性论证方法，不依赖于本征值或本征向量关于耦合常数的解析性假设。

Result: 证明了mCCZ4在弱耦合、满足锥分离条件的规范参数下，对Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力与四阶导数量子-张量理论均具有强双曲性；同时指出标准CCZ4在广义相对论中于穿刺规范下除两个缺陷退化情形外亦具强双曲性。

Conclusion: mCCZ4是一种适用于高阶引力理论稳定数值演化的强双曲公式，其数学适定性已获严格保障；该结果拓展了CCZ4框架的应用范围，并为更复杂引力模型的数值实现奠定理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that the modified CCZ4 (mCCZ4) formulation, used in nonlinear puncture-gauge simulations of (d+1)-dimensional Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity and four-derivative scalar-tensor theory in four dimensions, is strongly hyperbolic for both theories at sufficiently weak coupling, provided that the gauge parameters are chosen to satisfy a cone-separation condition. The proof applies to the full principal symbol of the theories, extending previous analyses based on first-order perturbation theory about the Einstein limit. We establish strong hyperbolicity by recasting the mCCZ4 equations into a formally equivalent second-order system whose derivative structure resembles that of the modified harmonic formulation of Kovács and Reall. This allows us to adapt their covariant characteristic analysis and continuity argument, without assuming that individual eigenvalues or eigenvectors depend analytically on the coupling. We also show that standard CCZ4 in general relativity is strongly hyperbolic in puncture gauge away from two defective degeneracies.

</details>


### [140] [Geometrothermodynamics characterization of ABG-AdS black hole immersed by string clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32907)
*Sayed Farooq Ahmed Shah,Safia Taj*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖特定热力学势的准齐次几何热力学框架，用于研究带弦云的正则Ayón-Beato–García反德西特（ABG-AdS）黑洞的热力学稳定性，揭示了响应函数异常与曲率奇点之间的精确对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规热力学稳定性判据依赖于热力学势的选择，而正则黑洞为研究热力学稳定性如何编码于时空几何中提供了自然场景。

Method: 构建了适用于ABG-AdS黑洞加弦云系统的、保持勒让德不变性的准齐次几何热力学框架；将熵、电单极荷和AdS半径作为满足广义准齐次条件的广延变量；引入三类热力学度规并分析其曲率奇点。

Result: 发现第一类度规曲率在零温极值边界发散（对应热容为零，Davies一阶相变），第二类在热容发散处发散（Davies二阶相变）；吉布斯自由能呈现燕尾结构，揭示小/大黑洞一阶相变及Hawking-Page型相变；弦云密度与电荷共同调控视界结构、允许热域与稳定性区间。

Conclusion: 几何热力学曲率奇点可统一刻画多种热力学相变，为理解黑洞热力学稳定性与时空几何的深层联系提供了新范式。

Abstract: Regular black holes, whose singular cores are replaced by smooth geometries sourced by nonlinear electrodynamics, provide a natural setting to study how thermodynamic stability is encoded in spacetime geometry. Conventional stability criteria, however, depend on the choice of thermodynamic potential. Here we develop the quasi-homogeneous geometrothermodynamics of the regular Ayón-Beato--García anti-de Sitter (ABG-AdS) black hole surrounded by a cloud of strings, a Legendre-invariant framework in which no potential needs to be preferred. We show that the entropy, electric monopole charge and AdS radius act as extensive variables obeying a generalized quasi-homogeneity condition, and that the equilibrium manifold then carries three families of thermodynamic metrics. We establish an exact correspondence between response-function anomalies and curvature singularities: the first metric's curvature diverges on the zero-temperature extremal boundary, where the heat capacity vanishes, signaling Davies first-order transitions, whereas the second's diverges precisely where the heat capacity does, pinpointing Davies second-order transitions. The Gibbs free energy displays a swallow-tail structure that reveals a first-order small/large black hole transition, whose metastable region is bounded by these divergences of the heat capacity, together with a Hawking--Page-type transition to thermal AdS. The string-cloud density and the electric charge jointly control the horizon structure, the admissible thermal domain and the stability intervals.

</details>


### [141] [The Cosmological Constant as a Clock and the Vilenkin Wavefunction as a Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32987)
*Stephon Alexander,Marcell Howard*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过协变的不可模重力理论解决量子引力中的时间问题，引入四维体积作为全局时钟，导出含时的Wheeler-DeWitt方程；构造了与Chern-Simons-Kodama态相关的精确背景无关解，证明了minisuperspace中的幺正演化，并指出Vilenkin波函数本质上是一种引力Gamow共振态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子引力中‘时间问题’这一基本难题，特别是如何在背景无关理论中定义物理时间并赋予波函数明确的动力学诠释。

Method: 采用协变形式的不可模重力（unimodular gravity），将宇宙学常数与全局四维时空体积变量视为共轭变量，以此构建内在引力时钟；结合Chern-Simons泛函与Wheeler-DeWitt方程，在minisuperspace中求解并分析边界条件。

Result: 获得精确、背景无关的解，验证幺正演化；揭示Vilenkin波函数是引力Gamow共振态，其非归一化源于出射边界条件；发现正宇宙学常数增大向膨胀分支隧穿的概率。

Conclusion: 为宇宙波函数提供了动力学诠释，澄清了边界条件的物理含义，表明四维体积可作为普适的内在时钟，推动量子宇宙学向可观测、可计算方向发展。

Abstract: We address the problem of time in quantum gravity using a covariant formulation of unimodular gravity. As a result, the cosmological constant is canonically conjugate to a global four-volume variable, which serves as a clock and leads to a time-dependent Wheeler--DeWitt equation. We construct exact, background-independent solutions related to the full Chern--Simons--Kodama state and demonstrate unitary evolution in minisuperspace. The Chern--Simons functional also provides an intrinsic gravitational clock. These solutions reveal that the Vilenkin wavefunction is a gravitational Gamow resonance, in which its non-normalizability follows from the outgoing boundary condition. The tunneling probability into the expanding branch increases with the positive cosmological constant. These results give a dynamical interpretation of the wavefunction of the universe and clarify the role of its boundary conditions.

</details>


### [142] [Scalar-tensor interactions selected through their first-order thermodynamic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33033)
*David S. Pereira*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了一阶热力学对标量-张量引力的限制能力，通过引入广义作用量并结合Eckart热传导与剪切粘滞方程，推导出有效流体量与几何剪切粘滞；发现热力学可积性条件能约束非线性Hessian相互作用，并与协变退化条件共同导致Horndeski理论子类的恢复，揭示了热力学可积性与动力学退化之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一阶热力学作为独立、互补的选择准则，用于筛选引力相互作用，特别是对标量-张量引力中高阶非线性项的物理合理性施加限制。

Method: 从含非线性曲率、爱因斯坦张量-黑塞耦合及标量黑塞二次/三次相互作用的广义作用量出发，推导完整有效流体量；同时施加Eckart热传导与剪切粘滞本构关系，并考虑相互作用间的抵消；保留完整热传导律，导出约束非线性Hessian项的热力学可积性条件；构造显式本构对并求解热演化；分析协变退化与标量幺正规范下退化对剩余相互作用的影响。

Result: 热力学可积性条件显著简化标量-度规动能结构；协变退化排除所有剩余非线性相互作用，恢复光速传播的Horndeski子类；而在标量幺正规范下退化时，部分非线性热力学相容相互作用仍可存活。

Conclusion: 一阶热力学不仅可作为引力理论构建的独立判据，还能与动力学退化等标准形成互补，为筛选物理合理的标量-张量引力模型提供新路径。

Abstract: We investigate the restrictive capability of first-order thermodynamics on scalar-tensor gravity. Starting from a general action containing nonlinear curvature dependence, an Einstein-tensor-Hessian coupling, and independent quadratic and cubic scalar-Hessian interactions, we derive the complete effective-fluid quantities. Simultaneously imposing Eckart's heat-conduction and shear-viscosity equations, while accounting for cancellations between interactions, substantially reduces the original operator basis and determines the geometric shear viscosity. Retaining the complete heat-conduction law, we establish an integrability condition that constrains the nonlinear Hessian interactions without prescribing the temperature and thermal conductivity. We construct explicit constitutive pairs and derive the associated thermal evolution, revealing contributions beyond the usual acceleration-aligned description. Remarkably, the thermodynamic integrability condition simplifies the scalar-metric kinetic structure such that imposing covariant degeneracy excludes the remaining nonlinear interactions, recovering the established luminal Horndeski subclass. Nonlinear thermodynamically compatible interactions nevertheless survive when degeneracy is restricted to the scalar-unitary gauge. These results establish a connection between thermodynamic integrability and dynamical degeneracy, demonstrating the potential of first-order thermodynamics as an independent and complementary criterion for selecting gravitational interactions.

</details>


### [143] [General-relativistic Vlasov disks in external magnetic fields around Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33053)
*Paweł Doruchowski,Tomasz Grewenda,David Kofroň,Patryk Mach*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在外部磁场中围绕克尔黑洞的无碰撞气体的广义相对论动力学模型，重点分析了赤道面上的薄盘，并推导出与气体相关的电流解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞周围等离子体在磁场中的行为，特别是其电流分布，对理解吸积盘物理和高能天体物理现象至关重要。

Method: 采用广义相对论框架下的动理学方法，构造依赖于粒子规范能量和角动量的分布函数，并在克尔及施瓦西度规下分别求解电流密度。

Result: 得到了赤道面薄盘中电流的解析表达式；在施瓦西背景下推广至更一般的能量-角动量依赖分布；并在Wald磁层中给出具体示例。

Conclusion: 该模型为分析磁场中黑洞吸积盘的电磁特性提供了理论基础，尤其适用于研究薄盘结构及其电流激发机制。

Abstract: We investigate stationary general-relativistic kinetic models of a collisionless gas around the Kerr black hole immersed in an external magnetic field. The main focus is put on razor-thin disks confined to the equatorial plane, but geometrically thick disks are also briefly mentioned. We consider distribution functions corresponding to constant canonical energy and constant canonical angular momentum of particles within the disk and obtain analytic expressions for the electric current associated with the gas. More general solutions characterized by distribution functions depending on the energy and the angular momentum are obtained assuming the Schwarzschild background metric. In explicit examples, we investigate disks in the Wald magnetosphere, describing an asymptotically uniform magnetic field.

</details>
